# 第 22 章　如是，众生欢笑不已

*4.2 版本主视觉　·　图源：米游社*

> So Laughed the Masses　·　如是，众生欢笑不已

## 183　Philosophy of the Tourist　观光客的哲学

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Duomension City　|　角色：Trailblazer; March 7th; Himeko; Welt; Pearl　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Real Holy Laughter!'' completed

### Story Recap

After defeating Sparxie at the Graphia Academy, I followed Himeko to the imagenesis room tucked away on the campus, where records of the Herald of Death incident were stored. There, we ran into Himeko's father, Ryusuke, and the two parted on bad terms after the conversation.

During a tour of the Express, the two put aside their differences temporarily. Afterward, we received an invitation from Pearl. She wanted to discuss a partnership with me as a fellow Supplicant and invited me to visit her company, Pearluxe Corp.

At Pearluxe Tower, we had a friendly spar with the superheroes, the Daybreak Squadron, and a tour of the mechatron production process. Pearl suggested that we join forces and host an Astral Express-themed convention, Star Rail FEST, to lure out the remaining Supplicants.

Meanwhile, Pearl also ordered Detective Ashveil to look for leads on the New Herald of Death case. The detective's relentless pursuit eventually led him to his prime suspect, the charity organization known as the Research Society of Happy Smiles.

Both Ashveil and Kuchiba witnessed the uncanny ability of Fulwish, president of the Smiles Society, to stir up a crowd. Realizing Fulwish was attempting to interfere in the Phantasmoon Games, the two paid a visit to Ashveil's old acquaintance, Cinderborne of the Daybreak Squadron.

During the conversation with Cinderborne, Ashveil sensed a deep, dark secret behind the Happiness Surgery that the Society offered to its members to erase their pain.

On the day of the Star Rail FEST, we were invited to the exhibition as special guests. During our visit, we ran into the mysterious Evanescia again. She also happened to be a hardcore fan of the comic series "The Fluffy," so we ended up exploring the festival dedicated to the Express, together.

However, as we passed through the Xianzhou Luofu exhibition area, the Daybreak Warriors challenged us, as Supplicants. Their provocation was accompanied by a smear campaign against me from Fulwish TV.

The Daybreak Warriors launched a massive attack with the reanimated mega-mechatron, Alloy King Pom-Pom. When all seemed lost, Ashveil unleashed his power and broke through the enemy's offensive. However, he also tore open a fissure in space during the process, which took everyone down into an unknown abandoned station.

The being hiding deep within the abandoned station stirred restlessly, twisting and ripping at space. Thankfully, Pearl was able to rescue us with her timely intervention. Little did we know, things were about to get worse with Ashveil's terrible revelation: The Happiness Surgery was actually implanting the flesh of Abominations of Abundance into Smiles Society members.

### Chat with your companions about the Phantasmoon in the sky

> *Huh? That winking moon up there in the sky... It just spoke! For real! Those eyes, without a doubt... Foretell something extraordinary!*

[Recap] You discussed the Phantasmoon Games' new stakes with Himeko and March 7th, concluding that it would bring even greater chaos to the universe. As curious bystanders gathered around you, Himeko suggested returning to the Express to continue the discussion.

*(Talk to March 7th)*

**Moon Gazers:** Wait, did you hear that? The moon just spoke!

**Moon Gazers:** I'm not hearing things, am I? The Master of Elation is here!

**Moon Gazers:** Hurry, make a wish! It might actually come true...

**March 7th:** Hang on... This isn't right... Is it?

**March 7th:** Whew— That winking moon up there in the sky... It just spoke! For real!

> **三月七：**这个、那个…这不对吧？

**March 7th:** (Trailblazer), it wasn't just me, was it? You heard that too, right!?

> **三月七：**呼——天上那个总是挤眉弄眼的月亮，竟然开口说话了！开拓者，不是我幻听吧，你也听到了，对吧？！

- ▸ I heard it, loud and clear.
- ▸ Everyone on the street heard it...
- ▸ The whole world heard it!

**Himeko:** I heard it, too...

> **姬子：**我也听到了……

**Himeko:** That's why I came straight back for you.

> **姬子：**所以，我立刻折返回来找你们了。

**Himeko:** "...The winner of these Phantasmoon Games shall inherit all my authority, and become the true, permanent Aeon of Elation."

> **姬子：**「…本次幻月游戏的胜者，将接过我所有的权柄，成为真正的、永久的欢愉星神。」

**Himeko:** Which means...

> **姬子：**这意味着……

- ▸ Aha changed the prize...
- ▸ The Aeon of Elation just raised the stakes?

**Himeko:** Precisely. Aha has raised the stakes. The final winner will inherit the authority of Elation itself.

> **姬子：**不错，阿哈加码了奖赏。最终的胜者会替代池接掌「欢愉」的权柄。

**March 7th:** Are you serious? This is so wild... it doesn't even feel real anymore.

> **三月七：**这…不可能吧？事情离谱到了一定程度，我都有些怀疑它的真实性了。

**Himeko:** Every time a Path has vanished or been absorbed, the cosmic turmoil has lasted for dozens of Amber Eras.

> **姬子：**银河中每一次命途消亡和吞并，产生的动荡都绵延了数十个琥珀纪。

**Himeko:** The transfer of an Aeon's divine authority... how could that ever amount to a mere wager, lightly traded in a single world's game?

> **姬子：**星神大权的更替，怎么可能只是某个星球一次游戏中被轻松转让的一枚注码？

- ▸ Why would Aha do something like this?

**Himeko:** The most logical explanation is that Aha wants to steer this game toward utter... insanity. But then, who can possibly fathom the mind of an Aeon?

> **姬子：**最合理的解释是，他希望让这场游戏的走向变得更加…疯狂。可谁又能揣度一位星神的思想？

- ▸ Have the stakes ever been raised like this before?

**Himeko:** Not every Phantasmoon Games's ending has been recorded. Many were lost to time, most likely a consequence of the winners exercising their newfound divine authority.

> **姬子：**并非每一场幻月游戏的结局都被记载了下来，历史上曾有大量隐没、被遗忘的幻月游戏，多半是胜者使用星神权柄时产生的连带结果。

**Himeko:** But as far as recorded history goes, this is unprecedented. After all, the chance to be an Aeon for just one minute is already an irresistible lure for many.

> **姬子：**但在有记载的历史里，这样的事情闻所未闻。毕竟「成为星神一分钟」，已经足够让许多人趋之若鹜。

- ▸ Something about this feels... off.

**March 7th:** Exactly, it's just too weird! Can you really just give up being an Aeon? What if this is just a big prank by those Masked Fools?

> **三月七：**是呀，怎么想都太奇怪了！星神的位置，难道是想让就能让的？会不会是那帮假面愚者耍的把戏？

**Himeko:** In the historical records, instances of the Phantasmoon speaking are few and far between, and the likelihood of it being influenced by an external force is exceedingly slim.

> **姬子：**历史记录中幻月开口说话的场合屈指可数，它被外力干涉的可能性微乎其微。

- ▸ What's the point of all this?

**Himeko:** Basically, unless what the Phantasmoon said is some cruel joke or a play on words... once the Games are over, a new god will be born, one of unknown identity, unknown morality, and unknown purpose.

> **姬子：**简单来说，如果幻月说的不是某个恶毒的玩笑或文字游戏。那么这场游戏胜负的骰子落定后，将会诞生一位不知其身份、不知其善恶、可能怀抱着任何目标的新神明。

**Himeko:** THEY won't be a guest with a one-minute pass to the "Elation" theme park. THEY will be its new owner.

> **姬子：**祂不是「欢愉」这座游乐场中得到一分钟体验券的客人，而是…整座游乐场的主人。

**Himeko:** THEY may spread good fortune and steer the great wheel of the cosmos toward hope... or THEY may be a god of utter malice, mocking all who stand against the Destruction, intent on dragging the universe into an abyss of despair.

> **姬子：**祂可能播撒好运将宇宙的巨轮导向充满希望的方向，也可能是一位彻头彻尾的恶神，恶意嘲弄所有对抗「毁灭」的人，意图将宇宙拖进绝望的渊薮。

- ▸ Maybe there's a silver lining here.

**March 7th:** You mean "A pessimist sees a half-empty glass, but an optimist sees a half-full one"?

> **三月七：**「悲观的人只看见半个空杯子，乐观的人看见了还有半杯水」，对吧？

**March 7th:** I knew you were an optimist, (Trailblazer)! Just like me. Besides, there's no way the Aeon of Elation would let their authority fall into unworthy hands! Right?

> **三月七：**你果然和我一样乐观，开拓者。再说了，欢愉星神怎么会坐视自己的权柄落入不配的人手里呢！对吧？

**March 7th:** Right... Right!?

> **三月七：**对…对的吧！

- ▸ The universe is headed for turmoil.

**March 7th:** Hey, don't you worry, (Trailblazer)! I'm here. I'll protect you!

> **三月七：**别怕，开拓者，有本姑娘在，会好好保护你的！

- ▸ Aha must have overheard my wish.

**Himeko:** You wished that if you won the Phantasmoon Games, "Aha would become the new leader of the Anti-Destruction Alliance"...

> **姬子：**你希望在游戏胜利后，「由阿哈担任反毁灭同盟的领袖」…

**Himeko:** Perhaps Aha heard your idea and decided to pack up and flee in the middle of the night...

> **姬子：**也许阿哈在听到这个想法后连夜收拾包袱逃走了……

**Excited Fan of "The Fluffy":** No way... it's Baseball Raccoon, March Bunny, and Himekat, in the flesh! What are they doing here?

> **兴奋的绒绒号粉丝：**我天，这是球棒浣熊、三月兔和姬奇猫本尊！她们怎么在这儿？

**Curious Passerby:** I couldn't even get a ticket to "Star Rail FEST," and now I'm seeing them right here on the street!

> **好奇的路人：**没抢到「星铁FES」的门票，没想到竟然转头在大街上看到了本人耶！

**March 7th:** Whoa... Where did all these people come from!?

> **三月七：**呜哇…突然间聚过来了这么多人！

**Curious Passerby:** Hey, that declaration the Phantasmoon mentioned was about you, right? What are you gonna do? Got any kind of "moon landing" plans in the works?

> **好奇的路人：**幻月发表的宣言提到你们了哎？你们会怎么回应啊？有制定什么「登月计划」吗？

**Excited Fan of "The Fluffy":** You should just hit it with your bat! Send that thing spinning into a black hole at the edge of the cosmos!

> **兴奋的绒绒号粉丝：**要不给它一球棒，把它滴溜溜地打到银河深处的黑洞里去吧！

- ▸ It's a bat, not a golf club.
- ▸ Why would I do that?

**Hopeful Passerby:** You smash anyone who stands in your way with that bat!

> **期待的路人：**用球棒把胆敢拦路的敌人通通揍翻！

**March 7th:** The way they say it makes (Trailblazer) sound like a supervillain...

> **三月七：**怎么说开拓者像是反派一样…？

**March 7th:** Uh-oh... You don't think that fight with the Daybreak Squadron made everyone turn on us, do you?

> **三月七：**糟了，打从和破晓战队交手后，大家是不是对咱们的看法有了变化……

**Irritable Passerby:** Come on, isn't that basically your trademark? You beat the Daybreak Warriors senseless!

> **没好气的路人：**你不一直都是这么做的吗！把破晓战士狠狠揍了一顿！

**Belligerent Passerby:** Ooh! All these big shots from across the cosmos, fighting to the death right in our backyard. It's gonna be epic!

> **好斗的路人：**好期待啊，银河里的大人物要在咱们的世界里斗个你死我活，实在是太精彩了！

**Concerned Passerby:** Epic? Are you brain-dead? One wrong move from them and we're in serious trouble! Just like what happened with the Herald of Death years ago!

> **担忧的路人：**精彩个屁！你脑袋里只有积水吗？这些人跺跺脚，咱们就要倒大霉！就像当年的告死魔一样！

**Autograph Seeker:** Sign this for me, future Aeon!

> **索要签名的看客：**给我个签名吧，未来的星神！

**Crowd of Onlookers:** Yo, when you become an Aeon, can I be your Emanator?

> **挤上来的看客：**喂，等你们当上星神，可以升我做令使吗？

**Himeko:** We should get away from this mess. Let's head back to the Express and discuss matters there.

> **姬子：**我们还是尽快离开这个是非之地为好。先回列车再说吧！

A moment later, as you squeeze your way through the crowd...

### Review the current state of Phantasmoon Games

> *Aha's raising the stakes? Things just got a whole lot more complicated. Anyway, better touch base with the crew and get everyone up to speed.*

[Recap] Pearl contacted you to report that, with Fulwish fanning the unrest, discussion among the residents about the Astral Express grew increasingly heated. Pearl advised the crew to stay out of sight and hoped Phantasmoon's new stakes would not get in the way of your cooperation.

**Welt:** You're back.

> **瓦尔特：**你们回来了啊。

**Welt:** Himeko messaged me. She filled me in on the Star Rail FEST and the Phantasmoon's declaration. Things really are changing fast.

> **瓦尔特：**「星铁FES」的事情，还有幻月的宣言，姬子都发消息告诉我了。事情变化得真快啊。

- ▸ Our allies may start to have second thoughts...

**Welt:** You're right. The fact that the Phantasmoon openly made a promise is anything but ordinary.

> **瓦尔特：**确实，「幻月开口许诺」这件事确实不同寻常……

- ▸ Now, winning the Games is more important than ever.
- ▸ I believe our objectives remain the same.

**March 7th:** Yeah, that's right. We came here to win. A bigger prize doesn't change that.

> **三月七：**说的不错。原本我们是为追求胜利而来，奖赏加码并不改变初衷。

**Welt:** Still, the mere fact that the Phantasmoon made a declaration is anything but ordinary...

> **瓦尔特：**但是，「幻月开口许诺」这件事本身却不同寻常……

**Himeko:** To prevent the divine authority from falling into the wrong hands, the Supplicants may go to any lengths now with their tactics and actions.

> **姬子：**为了避免神权落入错误的人手中，谒者们的战术和行动也许都会变得无所不用其极。

**Himeko:** After all, in most people's minds, they alone are the ones best suited to wield power. Right or wrong has little to do with it. Such is the nature of human will.

> **姬子：**毕竟，大部分人心目中，只有自己才是最能善用力量的人——无关对错，人的自我意志就是这种东西。

**Welt:** Yeah, and that may be exactly what the Phantasmoon wishes to see: a spectacular head-on battle of ambitions.

> **瓦尔特：**而这可能就是那轮幻月想要看到的，一场争锋相对的好戏。

**Communication Hint:** Madam Pearl has sent a communication request to the Astral Express. Accept transmission?

> **通信提示：**真珠女士向列车发来通信请求，请问是否接听？

**Pearl:** It hasn't been long since we parted, and here I am disturbing you once more. My sincerest apologies.

> **真珠：**才告别不久，又来叨扰诸位，实在抱歉。

**Himeko:** Please, there's no need to apologize. Given the urgency of this transmission, Madam Pearl, I assume you wish to discuss the Phantasmoon's recent declaration?

> **姬子：**不必客气，如此紧急的通讯，真珠女士是想谈谈刚才幻月发布的宣言？

**Pearl:** Nothing manipulates people's emotions quite like that moon in the sky. A permanent handover of divine authority... it is unprecedented.

> **真珠：**没有什么比天上那轮月亮更懂得拨弄人们的情绪了。永久移交神权…这样的事闻所未闻。

**Himeko:** I can't help but wonder whether some malicious presence lurks behind the Phantasmoon. Still... who else but the Elation THEMSELF could wield such control over it?

> **姬子：**我怀疑幻月背后是否隐藏着某个怀有恶意的存在。但…除了「欢愉」本尊，还有谁能操控幻月呢？

**Pearl:** In any case, the Phantasmoon's speech has made the public realize that the power to "create a god" is in their hands. Their emotions... and perhaps even their Wishpower... are swelling to historic levels. The Department of Aberration Defense even detected a surge of agitation and unrest among the imagenae.

> **真珠：**总之，幻月的发言让民众意识到自己手中掌握着「造神」的可能，他们的情绪…也许还有愿力正在空前高涨。「异常防御部」监测到了大量幻造种都在活化躁动。

**Pearl:** When normal people believe power rests in their hands, their decisions tend to grow more radical and reckless. Please be careful, and make sure to protect yourselves.

> **真珠：**当普通人认为自己权力在握时，他们的决策会变得更为激进、鲁莽。诸位，请谨慎对待，保护好自己。

- ▸ Protect ourselves?

**Pearl:** Correct. It would be wise to keep a low profile and stay out of the public eye.

> **真珠：**不错，尤其是尽量减少在公众场合抛头露面。

- ▸ We need protection?

**Pearl:** It isn't required, but I advise you to limit your appearances in public spaces.

> **真珠：**并不需要，但请尽量减少在公众场合抛头露面。

**Pearl:** The Media and Public Relations Department has been monitoring public sentiment. At present, speculation and criticism regarding the Astral Express are surging.

> **真珠：**「媒体宣传部」一直在监测舆情，如今人们对星穹列车物议沸腾。

**Welt:** I've seen it online myself. Following the fight with the Daybreak Warriors, there's been no shortage of hostile commentary aimed at (Trailblazer).

> **瓦尔特：**我在网上都看到了。和破晓战士交手后，冒出了不少对开拓者不利的言论。

**Welt:** A commentary program on TV has been claiming the Express's adventures are nothing but stories fabricated by the IPC.

> **瓦尔特：**电视台有时评栏目宣称，星穹列车的冒险全是公司一手包装的故事。

**Welt:** Many believe the Astral Express is oppressing the local heroes, and some are even speculating that (Trailblazer) is the new Herald of Death.

> **瓦尔特：**很多人认为星穹列车对本土英雄横加打压;还有人无端猜测开拓者就是新告死魔。

**March 7th:** What the—!? What's wrong with these people!?

> **三月七：**啥！这些人…怎么这么不讲理啊！

**Pearl:** These narratives are undoubtedly being amplified by media platforms steered by Fulwish.

> **真珠：**这些观点的背后，少不了满愿掌控的媒体推波助澜。

**Pearl:** I've received a report from the Security Bureau. They tell me that many fans of the Daybreak Squadron have been stirred up and are threatening retaliation against the Express...

> **真珠：**我接到了治安局的请示。他们告诉我，有不少破晓战队的粉丝受了煽动，宣称要对列车发起复……

**Pearl:** I'll reinforce security in case agitators attempt to storm the station.

> **真珠：**我会加派人力，以防好事者跑来冲击车站。

- ▸ You... can control public opinion, can't you?

**Pearl:** I can. But you overestimate what "control" really means. In the arena of public opinion, those who look strong are often the ones at a disadvantage. And besides—

> **真珠：**能，但你高估了「控制」这个词的作用：在舆论场中，强者才是弱势的一方。更何况——

**Himeko:** ...And besides, you'll be distancing yourself from us for a while, isn't that right?

> **姬子：**…更何况，您也会暂时与我们保持距离，我说的对吗？

**Pearl:** That is accurate. We intend to scale back our public association for the time being. If it's confirmed that the Express is "colluding with the IPC," its public image would only deteriorate further.

> **真珠：**您说的没错：是暂时减少「公开」的接触。相信各位能够理解：被坐实「与公司勾结」绝对会让列车的公众形象雪上加霜。

- ▸ Why don't we disclose what really happened in that fight?

**Pearl:** Regrettably, I cannot agree to this. Clarifying the facts amid a frenzy of public sentiment is like trying to quell boiling water by stirring it, it only fuels the flames.

> **真珠：**恕我拒绝。在舆论沸腾时解释真相，往往是扬汤止沸、火上浇油。

**Pearl:** "The Happiness Surgery causes recipients to undergo an Abundance Transformation." Disclosing such sensational information would probably be taken as a hoax, or trigger public panic.

> **真珠：**「幸福手术令受术者产生丰饶转变」——公开这样耸人听闻的信息多半会被视作谎言，又或是掀起群体恐慌情绪。

**Pearl:** I will instruct the Department of Aberration Defense to verify the true mechanism behind the Happiness Surgery as soon as possible and assess its potential impact.

> **真珠：**我会安排「异常防御部」尽快确认幸福手术的实际原理，评估它可能产生的影响。

**Pearl:** I strongly advise you to exercise restraint in front of the media. Even the smallest statement can be selectively edited and misrepresented.

> **真珠：**我奉劝诸位在面对镜头时也谨言慎行，你们的任何言论都有被剪辑和曲解的可能。

- ▸ This is all Fulwish's doing!

**Pearl:** That much is certain. Still, given the current volatility of public sentiment, please exercise caution and self-control.

> **真珠：**毫无疑问。但在这样敏感的舆论环境下，还请保持戒备和克制。

**Pearl:** I sincerely hope that the Phantasmoon's declaration will not disrupt the goodwill between us. A greater danger still stands before us: After listening to the declaration, who knows what extremes Fulwish might be driven to?

> **真珠：**我真诚希望：幻月的宣言不会干扰我们之间的友善合作。毕竟，大敌当前——满愿在听到幻月宣言后，又会做出怎样极端的行为呢？

**Himeko:** ...

> **姬子：**……

**Pearl:** Affairs demand my attention, so I must excuse myself. Should there be further developments, I will keep you apprised.

> **真珠：**诸务繁忙，诸位，我先告辞了。若后续还有情报，我会及时与你们分享的。

[Recap] Himeko speculated that the Abundance power Fulwish used to carry out the "Happiness Surgery" might have come from a predecessor Herald of Death. She proposed going to the Graphia Academy to comb through the sealed student records for clues about Fulwish.

**Himeko:** (Trailblazer), what do you make of the current situation?

> **姬子：**开拓者，你怎么看眼下的情况？

**(Trailblazer):** We need to take action and put a stop to Fulwish before things get even worse. Even if I'd never joined the Phantasmoon Games, I'd still make this decision.

> **开拓者：**在事情变得更糟前，我们也得行动起来，阻止满愿…就算从没加入幻月游戏，我也一定会这么做。

- ▸ For everyone she has fooled...

**(Trailblazer):** We cannot allow her to deceive and poison innocent people in the name of "happiness."

> **开拓者：**决不能任由她以「幸福」的名义，欺骗、荼毒无辜的普通人。

- ▸ For Skott...

**(Trailblazer):** Whatever her excuse is, killing others as if it's just a game is beyond forgiveness.

> **开拓者：**无论怀有怎样的理由，戏弄般地残杀任何人，都是不可接受的。

- ▸ For Cinderborne...

**(Trailblazer):** Cinderborne... and his friends only wished to become heroes, yet Fulwish toyed with them, trampled their dreams, and cast them aside as expendable pieces.

> **开拓者：**灰烬…还有他的朋友，怀着想成为英雄的心，却被满愿戏弄、践踏，成了被葬送的棋子。

**(Trailblazer):** As his idol. I have to protect him. I'll fight for him, and for everyone who believes in the Express.

> **开拓者：**作为他的偶像，我想保护他。我会为他…为所有喜欢列车的人战斗。

**Welt:** Agreed. Given the likelihood of Fulwish acting unpredictably, we should confront her with everything we have.

> **瓦尔特：**不错。满愿，作为目前最有可能失控的对手，确实应当全力应对。

**Welt:** That reminds me, I've been wondering. The "Happiness Surgery" taps into the power of Abundance, yet Fulwish seems perfectly normal. Just how did she get her hands on something so dangerous?

> **瓦尔特：**对了，我有个疑惑，幸研会为信众们施行的「幸福手术」利用了丰饶力量。但满愿看起来似乎只是个普通人，她到底从何处染指了这么危险的东西？

**Himeko:** Perhaps... it's the lingering poison left behind by the previous Herald of Death.

> **姬子：**也许，那是前代「告死魔」留下的遗毒。

- ▸ Herald of Death?
- ▸ Lingering poison?

**Himeko:** Fifteen years ago, the monster who turned living flesh into his artwork would, in the heat of combat, slip into bouts of frenzied elation and delirium.

> **姬子：**十五年前，那个能操控人类血肉…以之作画的魔鬼，在战斗中不时会陷入狂喜和澹妄。

**Himeko:** The murderous mark he left behind — the "Bloody Bloom" — was no mere delusion. According to his interrogation transcripts, he claimed to witness a brilliant golden tree promising him everlasting life.

> **姬子：**他所留下的杀人印记「绽放血手」，并不是出于臆想。在审讯记录里他供述，自己看到了一株光灿灿的金树，不断向他许诺永生。

- ▸ Golden tree...?
- ▸ ...Ambrosial Arbor?

**Himeko:** I cannot say whether the vision this madman claimed to see corresponds to something real. But the possibility cannot be ruled out.

> **姬子：**我并不清楚这个疯子臆想中看到的幻象是否真实对应着某物。但这确实有可能。

- ▸ The Phantasmoon Courtyard's sacred tree?

**Himeko:** I cannot say whether the vision this madman claimed to see corresponds to something real. But the possibility cannot be ruled out.

> **姬子：**我并不清楚这个疯子臆想中看到的幻象是否真实对应着某物。但这确实有可能。

**Himeko:** He claimed that in his hallucinations, the golden tree's branches coiled around him, drowning him in frenzy and pleasure. He wanted its blossoms to bloom before all, and to bring about that "miracle," he was willing to slaughter without restraint.

> **姬子：**他说自己在幻觉中被那株金树的枝条所攀缠，被狂喜和快感淹没。他想让这棵树的花朵在所有人面前绽放——为了实现这一奇迹，他愿意大开杀戒。

**Himeko:** In hindsight, it's clear that the Herald of Death was a Supplicant who bore the power of Abundance. The tragedy he orchestrated in Planarcadia ultimately forged another version of himself... Fulwish.

> **姬子：**如今回头看来，告死魔显然是一名身负丰饶力量的谒者。他在二相乐园制造的悲剧，炮制出了另一个自己…满愿。

**Himeko:** Right now, in Planarcadia, how many have undergone the Happiness Surgery and turned into potential Abominations of Abundance? It's a terrifying thought.

> **姬子：**此时此刻，二相乐园里有多少人接受了幸福手术，成了潜在的丰饶孽物？想想真让人不寒而栗啊。

**Himeko:** Fulwish... what has happened to you over the years? Hiding behind these innocent people... what are you really trying to accomplish?

> **姬子：**满愿…这些年在你身上发生了什么？躲在这些无辜的人身后，你到底想做什么？

**Himeko:** I need more information about this junior of mine. I plan to head to Graphia Academy. Care to join me?

> **姬子：**我必须查清楚更多关于这位学妹的信息。我想去绘世学院一趟，你们要不要和我一起行动？

**Himeko:** If we're to investigate Fulwish, we need to examine the traces left behind from the Herald of Death incident that took place during the last Phantasmoon Games... the recollections of teachers, and the student records sealed away by the school. We're bound to find a vital clue there.

> **姬子：**要调查满愿，上一次幻月游戏中告死魔事件留下的痕迹、任教老师的回忆、学校封存的学生档案…从中一定能找出什么有意义的线索。

- ▸ Let's find out everything about Fulwish!

**Himeko:** If you're set, let's get moving.

> **姬子：**如果准备好，我们就立刻出发。

- ▸ I need a moment to prepare. I'll catch you at the academy.

**Himeko:** Mm-hmm. Fulwish isn't an opponent to be taken lightly, we'll need to be thoroughly prepared. See you at the academy.

> **姬子：**嗯，满愿不是一个好应付的对手，确实得做足准备才是。学校见。

## 184　In the Shadow of the Millennium　在千禧年的阴影下

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Graphia Academy　|　角色：Trailblazer; March 7th; Himeko; Ryusuke; Asat Pramad; Dollie; Froggy Whiteboard; Fulwish; Belle; Poofy; Snowie; Welt　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Philosophy of the Tourist'' completed

### Head to Graphia Academy and continue your adventure

> *Petals that grace the branches are not always blessings of new life. They may well be the curse of Abundance. What really went down in the Phantasmoon Games fifteen years ago...*

*(Talk to Welt Yang, optional)*

**Welt:** (Trailblazer), Fulwish won't let up her media offensive. Watch your backs out there.

**Welt:** I'll remain aboard the Express and manage things from here. I'll provide support if needed.

- ▸ I'll leave things on the Express to you. It's in good hands.
- ▸ You be careful too, Mr. Yang.

**Welt:** Don't you worry about me. I've still got plenty of fight left in me yet.

[Recap] You and Himeko, along with March 7th, arrived at the Graphia Academy. Since you needed to look into records from fifteen years ago, Himeko proposed obtaining Dean Ryusuke's approval first. With that decided, you headed for the dean's office.

*(Go to Graphia Academy)*

**March 7th:** Ahh, well, after that so-called "dream campus experience," we've ended up back at Graphia Academy. It's almost like revisiting the place after graduation.

> **三月七：**啊，在经历了那场「梦幻校园生活」以后，又回到了绘世学院。有种毕业生故地重游的感觉。

- ▸ Wake up, March...
- ▸ I suppose you could call this your alma mater now.

**March 7th:** Wait a minute, this nostalgia isn't an aftereffect of the Sparxie Virus, r—right!?

> **三月七：**等等，这种怀念的心情该不会是火花病毒的后遗症吧？！

**Himeko:** Don't worry, March. The Department of Aberration Defense conducted a "comprehensive disinfection" of the campus.

> **姬子：**别担心，小三月。异常防御部已经对学校做了「全面消毒」。

**March 7th:** That's a relief. The last thing I need is to be mid-investigation and suddenly morph into some hopping, dancing mushroom rabbit...

> **三月七：**那就好，我可不想查着查着突然变成一只跳着舞的兔子蘑菇……

**March 7th:** Speaking of which, where should we begin our investigation into Fulwish?

> **三月七：**说起来，关于满愿，我们该从哪儿开始查起啊？

- ▸ Maybe we should start with the teachers who are familiar with the situation?

**Himeko:** I think it's best we see my father first.

> **姬子：**我想先去见一见我的父亲。

- ▸ We could check the archives.
- ▸ How about talking to the dean first?

**March 7th:** Should we go and find Mr. Ryusuke first?

> **三月七：**先去找隆介先生吗？

**Himeko:** It's only appropriate that we obtain permission from the dean before proceeding with the investigation.

> **姬子：**要在学校调查，于情于理，还是先征得校长本人的同意为好。

**Himeko:** Fifteen years have passed, but there should be a backup of the student files, work logs, or visitor records, and only he knows where they're stored.

> **姬子：**虽然已经十五年过去了…但当年的学籍档案、工作日志和随访记录应该有备份幸存。这些东西存放的地方，也只有他能告诉我们了。

**Himeko:** Let's go then. To the dean's office.

> **姬子：**走吧，我们先去校长室。

### Greet Himeko's father

> *You ran into the dean... in the dean's office!? You clearly remember that in the last turn of the "Phantasmoon Games," Dean Sparxie was never around whenever you needed her signature. Wait, those were just false memories fabricated by Sparxie. Come to think of it, Dean Ryusuke seemed to have mistaken you for Himeko's child earlier. ...Somehow, the atmosphere just got super awkward.*

[Recap] Ryusuke praised your showing against the Daybreak Squadron, then asked you and March 7th to keep Himeko safe. He added that the relevant materials had already been organized in the archive room, and that you could also speak with teacher Ms. Dollie.

*(Approach Ryusuke)*

**Ryusuke:** Himeko! There you are at last!

> **隆介：**姬子！你终于出现了啊！

**Ryusuke:** The news said you got into a huge fight with those... what were they called... warriors at the floating isle exhibition, and then you disappeared! Let me see, you're not hurt anywhere, are you?

> **隆介：**新闻上说你们和那什么什么战士在浮岛的展会上大打出手，然后又失踪了！快让我看看，没在哪儿磕着碰着吧？

**Ryusuke:** I sent you a dozen messages and you didn't reply to a single one! Do you have any idea how worried I was!?

> **隆介：**我说！我可是给你发了十几条消息，你居然一条都没回我！你脑袋里是怎么想的啊？

**Himeko:** ...

> **姬子：**……

**Ryusuke:** But seeing that you've come to tell me you're safe in person, I'll... let this slide!

> **隆介：**不过，看在你跑上门来报平安，这事…就算了！

**Ryusuke:** If you're really so preoccupied, a message or a call would do, you know...

> **隆介：**你真忙的话，回条消息或者打个电话就好……

**Ryusuke:** Ah, it's you two. I saw you on the news, as well. Valiantly fought! Hah, these mechatron Warriors don't know who they're messing with, to think they dared challenge my daughter's subordinates.

> **隆介：**两位，我在新闻里看到了你们的英姿，打得很不错！呵呵，这些什么机铠战士，毛头小子不自量力，居然敢向我女儿的手下发起挑战。

**March 7th:** Now it feels like we're the underlings of Warlord Himeko or something...

> **三月七：**这话说的，咱们俩感觉就像是姬子大魔王的爪牙……

**Ryusuke:** In any case, I'll leave Himeko in your hands. Please, try to keep her out of combat.

> **隆介：**总之，保护姬子的工作就拜托你俩了，可别让她真的上阵。

- ▸ Don't worry, I'm a great bodyguard!
- ▸ Of course, grandpa! I got it, grandpa!

**Himeko:** Stop, stop! Every time you and (Trailblazer) get together, the conversation somehow drifts completely off course...

> **姬子：**停、停！每次你和开拓者碰在一起，话题总会不知道飘到哪儿去……

**Himeko:** I'm fine, I'm good. Your daughter's no porcelain doll. We came here to—

> **姬子：**我很好，我没事，你的女儿可不是什么弱不禁风的瓷娃娃。我们这一次来——

**Ryusuke:** I know. I understand... You didn't come as my "daughter," rather, I am currently speaking to the navigator of the Astral Express.

> **隆介：**我懂，我懂…你不是以「女儿」的身份来这里的，此刻站在我面前的是「星穹列车的领航员」。

**Ryusuke:** But tell me... has there been progress on the new Herald of Death case? I wasn't expecting the questioning to come in rounds.

> **隆介：**不过…新告死魔的案子是又有什么进展了吗？轮番审讯来得真是突然。

**March 7th:** In rounds?

> **三月七：**轮番审讯？

**Ryusuke:** The Department of Aberration Defense also sent someone here before you. They inquired about Fulwish...

> **隆介：**在你们到来前，异常防御部也派人来问了不少问题，其中还有关于满愿的事……

**Ryusuke:** But regrettably, I truly don't know how to answer. On one hand, I wasn't here fifteen years ago; on the other, she is still considered one of our school's distinguished alumni.

> **隆介：**但很遗憾，我实在不知道该怎么回答。一方面，十五年前，我并不在这里；另一方面，她好歹也算是我校的杰出校友。

**Ryusuke:** I'm aware that you're involved in the investigation too, so I compiled the pertinent documents ahead of time. I hope they'll be of use to you.

> **隆介：**我知道你们也被牵扯进了这案子，所以提前把相关资料整理出来了，希望你们能用到。

**March 7th:** Thank you, Mr. Ryusuke.

> **三月七：**谢谢你，隆介先生。

**Ryusuke:** Go ahead. The files are inside the archive room, take whatever materials you need.

> **隆介：**去吧去吧，资料都存放在档案室里了，去找对你们有用的东西吧。

- ▸ Oh right, about Fulwish...

**Ryusuke:** To be honest, it wasn't until the Herald of Death incident fifteen years ago that I first learned her name.

> **隆介：**说实话，我是在十五年前的告死魔事件之后，才知道她名字的。

**Ryusuke:** As the dean, I should've met up with her more often and tried harder to soothe the wounds it left on her. But I... I just couldn't.

> **隆介：**身为校长，我理应和她多见几面，试着平复她内心的创伤。可惜…我实在是做不到。

- ▸ Why?

**Ryusuke:** Do you really need all the details? The whole "serving as a model example" thing... I don't want to tarnish it completely.

> **隆介：**真要问得这么清楚吗？「为人师表」这四个字，我也不想糟蹋得太彻底啊……

**Ryusuke:** I knew it was a selfish thought, but I just couldn't help it. Back then, whenever I heard her name, I thought to myself—

> **隆介：**我知道这想法是多么自私，但就是控制不住，那段时间只要听到这个名字，我就不由得会想——

**Ryusuke:** If someone was able to survive the incident, then why couldn't it have been my daughter?

> **隆介：**既然有人能够幸存下来，为什么就不能是我的女儿呢？

**Himeko:** ...

> **姬子：**……

- ▸ Fifteen years ago, you...

**Ryusuke:** Fifteen years ago, I received a commission to paint a massive portrait for an Elixir Seekers chief on the planet of Goliath's Shepherd. They said he had an elixir that could cure countless diseases...

> **隆介：**十五年前，我收到委托，前往歌利亚牧者星为某位求药使酋长绘制巨幅地画肖像。据说，他有一种能治愈无数疾病的灵药……

**Ryusuke:** By the time I came back, everything had changed. There had been a terrible accident on campus, and my daughter...

> **隆介：**但当我回来的时候，一切都变了。学校出了天大的祸事，女儿也……

**Himeko:** ...

> **姬子：**……

**Ryusuke:** Even though I knew my presence here wouldn't have changed anything, because of that incident, I made a promise to never leave this world again during the Phantasmoon Games.

> **隆介：**虽然我知道自己就算当时身在此间，也没法改变什么。但那件事让我下定决心，如果有生之年再遇上一回幻月游戏，一定不能离开这个世界。

**Ryusuke:** That's why I immediately flew here when I heard about the full Phantasmoon. But once again, I was too late, and the Supplicant called Sparxie had turned the campus upside down.

> **隆介：**所以，这一次听说幻月满盈，我就立刻飞来了。可惜还是慢上一步，学校还是给那个叫火花的谒者搅得天翻地覆。

**March 7th:** It's alright, you can leave the crisis-solving to the Nameless.

> **三月七：**解决危机这样的麻烦事，还是交给咱们无名客吧。

**Ryusuke:** You're right about that. Yet oddly, once Sparxie stirred up all that chaos, applications to the school soared to an all-time high. The times we live in are increasingly beyond my comprehension.

> **隆介：**你说的没错。但说来也奇怪，火花一通大闹之后，学校的报考意向居然突破了历史新高。这个时代，越来越让我读不懂了。

- ▸ We won't take up any more of your time, then.

**Ryusuke:** You're always welcome. Other than the archive room, you could also try talking to Ms. Dollie. She's the longest-serving faculty member here and likely knows the most.

> **隆介：**不必客气。除了档案室外，你们也可以和啵啵娃老师谈谈，她在这座学校资历最久，知道的事情也最多。

**Himeko:** Then we'll try the archive room. Let's go, you two.

> **姬子：**那我们先去档案室了。两位，我们走吧。

**Himeko:** Thank you, Dad.

> **姬子：**谢谢你，爸爸。

### Find the teacher Ms. Dollie and continue the investigation

> *Fulwish, the people's beloved "daughter," head of Seafeld TV, leader of the Happiness Cult, survivor of the Bloodstained Games fifteen years ago... There are still many unanswered questions about her. Rally your companions to uncover what she has been through over the past fifteen years.*

**March 7th:** We should be able to find the archive room and Ms. Dollie around... the Imagenesis classroom, right?

> **三月七：**档案室和啵啵娃老师…应该都在幻造教师附近吧？

*(Talk to Ryusuke again, optional)*

**Ryusuke:** You're always welcome. Other than the archive room, you could also try talking to Ms. Dollie. She's the longest-serving faculty member here and likely knows the most.

[Recap] According to Ms. Dollie, Fulwish had been an outstanding student who, after graduation, set out alone to continue her studies beyond the sky. When she returned, she revitalized Seafeld TV, often came back to visit the school, and funded many scholarship initiatives.

*(Approach Dollie)*

**Dollie:** Oh my goodness, I thought I recognized you in the distance. Himeko, it really is you!

> **啵啵娃：**啊呀呀，远远就看到姬子同学了，走近一看，果然是你！

**Himeko:** Ms. Dollie! Please forgive me for not coming to see you sooner. I've been back for a while now, but this is the first chance I've had to come see you.

> **姬子：**老师！回故乡这么久，还是第一次能抽空来拜访您，实在抱歉。

**Dollie:** Don't worry about it. I'm simply happy that you cared enough to visit!

> **啵啵娃：**不碍事，你有心回来看望，我就很高兴啦！

**Dollie:** I must apologize about the mask you entrusted me. In the end...

> **啵啵娃：**咳咳，着实抱歉，姬子同学托付保管的面具，最后还是……

**Himeko:** Please, I can't thank you enough for safeguarding the mask all those years.

> **姬子：**您能替我保管面具这么久，我已经很感激了。

**Dollie:** Hehe, look at you, you haven't changed a bit. You and your friends look like you're in a hurry. Is there something you need to attend to?

> **啵啵娃：**呵呵，你还是和以前一样。看你们急匆匆的模样，这次回学校，是有什么事要办吗？

- ▸ We're here to ask about Fulwish.
- ▸ It is said Fulwish is an outstanding alumna.

**Dollie:** Fulwish...? The poor child who survived the Herald of Death?

> **啵啵娃：**满愿…？那个从告死魔手里幸存下来的可怜孩子？

**Dollie:** The school organized a fundraiser for her back then. I still remember how she bowed again and again with tears in her eyes at everyone who helped.

> **啵啵娃：**当年，学校为她组织了募捐。她抹着眼泪，拼命向大家鞠躬的样子，我现在还记得啊。

**Dollie:** Poor girl. Though she was lucky to have survived, her parents... didn't make it. There were rumors flying around the school saying she had something to do with the Herald of Death. Otherwise, why would she be so lucky? She's the only one who survived... What a bunch of scoundrels!

> **啵啵娃：**可怜啊，虽然运气好，活了下来，却听说她的父母都…遭难了。学校里有流言，传她和告死魔有联系，不然怎么会运气这么好，偏偏就她幸免了…真是一群嘴上不积德的坏东西！

**Dollie:** She had good grades and a good heart. After becoming famous, she often came back to visit the school, and sponsored a number of scholarship projects.

> **啵啵娃：**这孩子读书成绩好，心眼也很好。她功成名就后，也时常回学校探望，还资助了不少助学金项目。

**March 7th:** Speaking of which, do you know anything about what happened to her after she graduated from school?

> **三月七：**说起这个，她在学校毕业后的经历，您知道什么消息吗？

**Dollie:** I heard she went somewhere beyond the sky to further her studies after graduation. She had the chance to work for the Interastral Peace Corporation, but chose to return home and take over Seafeld TV instead, which was on the verge of bankruptcy.

> **啵啵娃：**听说大学毕业后，她独自去往了天外进修。明明有加入星际和平公司的机会，却选择了回到故乡，接手濒临倒闭的海原电视台。

**Himeko:** Somewhere beyond the sky... Ms. Dollie, do you know where she went exactly?

> **姬子：**「天外进修」…老师，您知道她具体的去处吗？

**Dollie:** I never heard. However, she pushed out many projects after taking over the TV station, and managed to expand their business to the whole of Planarcadia in just a few years. That is all I know.

> **啵啵娃：**这倒是从未听说过。不过，接手电视台后，她连番推出了不少企划，没用几年，就把业务扩张到了整个二相乐园。老身知道的，也就是这些了。

**Himeko:** Thank you, Ms. Dollie, for your intel.

> **姬子：**多谢，老师，感谢您的情报。

**Dollie:** Yet another cycle of the Phantasmoon Games... Himeko, please take care of yourself.

*(Talk to Dollie again, optional)*

**Dollie:** Yet another cycle of the Phantasmoon Games... Himeko, please take care of yourself.

*(Listen to the students, optional)*

**Apprehensive Student:** The new Herald of Death is real! They say he came back to life and was responsible for what happened in Dovebrook!

> **忧心忡忡的学生：**新告死魔的事情居然是真的！他们说鸽川那桩案子就是告死魔死而复活做的！

**Apprehensive Student:** He's not coming here to kill again, i—is he? Maybe we should ask for leave and stay home until this blows over?

> **忧心忡忡的学生：**这一次他该不会再来咱们学校大开杀戒吧？要不请个长假，回家避避风头吧？

Class of 1984 Alumni Yearbook: Fulwish

**Personal Information:** Name: Fulwish Birthday: ███, ██th Address: No. █, ███████ Street, Seafeld City, Planarcadia, Consternation Starzone Contact: ███████████ Post-Graduation Destination: Institute of Cognitive Science, Garden Star of Botanical Study My Interests Favorite Color: White Dream Destination: The Seaside Favorite Animal: Hallucigenia Ideal Career: Teacher Messages from Classmates Graduation day has finally arrived. But so many never made it back. If I hadn't slipped away early that day... could I have done something for them? When I look at the empty seats in our classroom, I often feel guilty for being powerless. Fulwish, did you feel the same? But I've come to terms with it now. The past is the past. Those of us left behind... we have to take care of ourselves. I hear the climate on Garden Star of Botanical Study is very different. Take care of yourself, Fulwish. — Sia Winning first place in the campus imagenesis competition, ranking at the top for several consecutive semesters... You have always been outstanding, Fulwish. That's why I truly believe you can make it through that nightmare and show all of Planarcadia, with a smile, that we won't be easily defeated by a single tragedy. I've never doubted that. A new chapter in life is about to begin. Facing the lens of life, please keep on smiling! — Sara Message from a Teacher Hearing that you will soon leave Planarcadia to pursue cognitive science, I can't help but feel a sense of loss. It's a failure on my part as a teacher that I couldn't inspire you to stay in the imagenesis industry. But as your teacher, I fully understand your choice. To have helped shape a researcher like you for the Intelligentsia Guild fills me with deep pride. I sincerely wish you the vastest of horizons, Fulwish. May you realize your dreams, and may the sorrow sown in the past blossom into flowers of happiness in the future. — Hermien Message from Parents *Left blank*

> **满愿：**所以，我做了个决定，撕开它！我要让这底下所有血淋淋的、没法被忘掉的一切…重见天日！

## 185　A Superhero Caught in a Moral Dilemma　超级英雄的道德困境

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Phantasmoon Courtyard　|　角色：Evanescia; Yao Guang; Sunday; Meowton; Dan Heng; Blade; Ashveil　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''In the Shadow of the Millennium'' completed

### Discuss the next plan

> *I've witnessed countless unforgettable tales, countless twists and turns of fate. Those "heroes," those "Supplicants," contributed so many spectacular performances to this game. They take their curtain call, then step back onto the stage... Such stories have repeated themselves a thousand times over, ever since the very first, all for the sake of this "arcadia." I've never grown weary: deconstructing, arranging, combining... you, always so similar, yet creating stories that are utterly different. I await your performance in this game. And so, whether it be this "Phantasmoon Games" or this "arcadia," it needs the right guidance.*

[Recap] Evanescia showed Yao Guang her Supplicant's mask, saying that for someone in her role as an arbitrator, this was unprecedented, and that whoever had caused it remained unknown. Yao Guang proposed that they work together in the Phantasmoon Games to track down the culprit and asked her to release the crow in the secret courtyard, through which Sunday then sent information about Fulwish and suggested that the two of them head to Seafeld City.

Not long before, in the Phantasmoon Courtyard...

**Yao Guang:** You just said: "As for the brewing storm... Whether the turmoil once again is the result of The Laughter's mischief" ...What exactly was that supposed to mean?

> **爻光：**千万次卜卦后，纵然能心如止水，但每一次预见恶兆，心湖仍难免暗生晦云。

**Yao Guang:** You mean to say the Joyseeker wants to destroy the very rules THEY laid down, the game THEY wove with THEIR own hand?

> **爻光：**因此在占卜前，我通常会抚拭心镜，摒除杂念，以免结果受到干扰。

**Evanescia:** Come over here.

**Evanescia:** This fox mask is a ritual instrument I once used in dance and music. It accompanied me across vast distances in search of an elixir of immortality, a mark of who I was in life.

> **爻光：**及时行乐，是啊，可人间之乐如朝露易逝，而我等职责却似重山难移。

**Evanescia:** I've lost count of how long it has been since I last wore it since being enlightened and transplanted here. But here it is, presented upon the altar again. Which means...

> **绯英：**卜者常因预见明日的苦难，而错失眼前的乐趣。但你有想过吗，那些所谓的明日之苦，或许正是你今日恐惧种下的毒果？

**Yao Guang:** As the game's referee, you've been pushed onto the field. In this moment, you are now a Supplicant.

**Evanescia:** For over a century, I presided over a war game in which Supplicants carved out their own territories and vied for supremacy using the power of the Masks.

> **绯英：**正因为知道了结局，通往它的每一帧风景才如此珍贵、独一无二，不是吗？

**Evanescia:** I've also hosted survival games where Emanators took the stage to compete, invoking Aeonic powers to try and slaughter every last opponent in a single day.

> **绯英：**你看，眼前这片港湾，在第一次踏足时，这些街道、渔船和行人都还不存在呢。

**Evanescia:** Whenever the Phantasmoon Games are at risk of being suspended or disrupted, I will use the authority granted by the Elation and the Abundance to measure, judge, and punish any violators.

**Evanescia:** I guess you could call me the "security protocol" for the Phantasmoon Games. But now, someone has gone and designated me as a "Supplicant"... It's utterly unheard of.

> **绯英：**大海被汽化，只留下开裂的海床和遮天蔽日的灰烬。界外天魔的军团尚未离去，而涌入鸽川的浪花，那时也还没有到来。

**Evanescia:** Who else but The Laughter could pull off something like this?

> **绯英：**没错，按这个说法，吃糖吃到蛙牙也算幸福咯？反正过程是甜的。

**Yao Guang:** Enjoy the present... Indeed. Yet, worldly joys are fleeting as morning dew, while our duties are as immovable as heavy mountains.

**Evanescia:** It is precisely because we know the ending that every scene along the way is so precious and unique, isn't it?

**Evanescia:** As far as the eye could see... there was only fire.

**Evanescia:** The seas had been vaporized, leaving only fractured seabeds and ash that blotted out the sun. The demonic legions from beyond still hadn't departed, and the waves that would surge into Dovebrook had, in that moment, not yet arrived.

**Evanescia:** Just on the mountain over there, a painter and I looked out at the iron-red scars on the earth. She told me, "That which vanishes is bound to return." She swore to use her brush to preserve everything claimed by the Destruction.

**Yao Guang:** It seems she succeeded.

**Evanescia:** And so, I too shall keep my promise to her, maintaining the course of the Phantasmoon Games until the sands of time slip away.

**Evanescia:** With every descent into this game's cycles, I shall enjoy myself to the fullest. Like sipping tea, be it sweet or bitter, I shall savor the flavor of every instant. To do otherwise would be a disservice to the people and things that have passed.

**Yao Guang:** To savor a fleeting moment against the backdrop of eternity... naturally, the flavor is profound. A pity, though. Even as a long-life species, my time is brief compared to yours. As a Xianzhou native, the karma I carry is far too heavy. I could never learn how to be as... carefree as you.

**Evanescia:** We're almost there.

[Recap] Evanescia's sacred vessel is kept on a floating island concealed by heavy fog and woods. Whenever Evanescia plays her music, the fog disperses.

*(Approach mission target)*

**Yao Guang:** Is this it? You keep the sacred instrument out here in broad daylight? Aren't you worried someone might make it disappear?

> **爻光：**到了？你把法器存放在这光天化日之下，就不怕它不翼而飞？

**Evanescia:** Hold your horses. We've still got a ways to go. As for my precious measuring instrument, do you really think I'd just leave it lying around in the middle of nowhere?

> **绯英：**急什么，离目的地还有一段路。我那宝贝丈量之器，怎么会随便扔在这荒郊野外？

**Yao Guang:** With all due respect, there is no path ahead.

> **爻光：**恕我直言，前方已无路可走。

**Yao Guang:** I get it. It's probably just an optical illusion. Extraordinary beings like you always love pulling that "the path only opens to the destined" trick. What's next? A secret passphrase? Do I find a stone matrix and jump through it? Or maybe...

> **爻光：**我懂了，多半是些障眼法。像你们这样的非凡存在，总喜欢玩些「路不遇有缘人不开」的把戏。接下来是要对暗号？找个石头围成的阵法跳进去？还是说……

**Yao Guang:** Do you sing a little song, wave your hand, and the trees just obediently stand up and split into two neat rows?

> **爻光：**你唱首歌儿，手一挥，树就乖乖站起来分两边排排坐好？

**Evanescia:** I'm no Woof Woof Dan... I don't have that kind of skill.

> **绯英：**我不是汪汪丹…我可没这样的本事。

**Evanescia:** A long time ago, I raised a floating island right here for someone to paint.

> **绯英：**许久之前，我曾在这儿升起过一座飞岛供人作画。

**Evanescia:** To prevent others from wandering in by mistake, I asked that artist to paint thick fog and woodland here, so passersby wouldn't stray inside.

> **绯英：**为了防止旁人误入其中，我拜托那位绘师在这里画出一片茫茫大雾与树林，好让路过的人不至于误入其中。

**Evanescia:** We made a deal: As long as I play my song, the fog will disperse.

> **绯英：**我和那人约定，只要我奏响乐声，雾气便会散去。

### Play the melody and dispel the mist

> *Work hard and stay focused, then good fortune will come naturally. Hoot, I still can't do it? Once I'm done with this, I'm gonna lock myself inside and binge Fluffy Across the Blue for a whole week!*

*(Enter Interplanar Jump!)*

**Yao Guang:** That's a cute puppy. Looks like it's taken a liking to you.

> **爻光：**可爱的小狗，它看起来挺喜欢你呢。

(Activate lever)

**Evanescia:** I'll just borrow this cutie's head for a sec.

> **绯英：**借小可爱狗头一用。

(Jump up with the trampoline)

**Evanescia:** Here we are.

> **绯英：**走吧。

Didn't expect this instrument to have such a childlike charm.

Hmm... I remember the music score being ↖↑←↖↓↙→↘.

(If the wrong Bizarre Melody is played)

Hey, don't tell me you're tone-deaf?

### Step onto the floating isle and retrieve the ruler blade

> *"Stream forth... the gleam of old blades..." Oops, my bad! I was just trying to quote a classic line from Volume 3 of Fluffy Across the Blue. This really is just an ordinary ruler, isn't it? Of course it is.*

*(Go up the stairs)*

**Evanescia:** What do you think? It's beautiful, isn't it?

> **绯英：**怎么样，很美吧？

**Yao Guang:** Compared to the Yuque's scenery... I suppose it's more natural.

> **爻光：**与预期的景色比…也就胜在天然吧。

**Evanescia:** Would it kill you to pay a compliment?

> **绯英：**你这人，夸一句会死吗？

**Yao Guang:** Yes.

> **爻光：**会。

[Recap] Evanescia obtained the "Spiritbranch of True Law." However, the "measuring" revealed that Fulwish was not a Supplicant. Instead, behind this mask stood countless Supplicants scattered across Planarcadia, rendering any judgment impossible. Meanwhile, Dan Heng, Blade, and Ashveil arrived on the scene. Blade stated that he had come to slay their mutual enemy. The Abundance cells used in the Happiness Surgery had originated from Shuhu, the Emanator of Abundance. The one wearing the Octomorph Mask was none other than Shuhu, who had been dismembered and was in fact parasitizing the bodies of Planarcadia's surgery recipients.

*(Approach mission target)*

**Yao Guang:** ...

> **爻光：**……

**Evanescia:** What are you laughing at?

> **绯英：**你笑什么？

**Yao Guang:** My apologies, I didn't mean to mock. I was merely curious... Is this thing really the "sacred instrument" with which you pass judgment upon Supplicants? Is it really just... a literal ruler?

> **爻光：**失礼了，并无嘲笑之意。我只是好奇，这就是你执之以裁决谒者的「法器」？居然真是字面意思的…尺子？

**Evanescia:** Hey, don't underestimate this "Spiritbranch of True Law"! When it first appeared in the form of a blade 150 years ago it could cleave a mountain in half with a single effortless swing!

> **绯英：**你可别小瞧了这「真律灵枝」，一百五十年前它以刀之姿态现身人前时，可是能在轻松一挥间拦腰斩断大山的！

**Yao Guang:** Miss Evanescia, let's keep such fanciful tales between us, shall we...

> **爻光：**绯英小姐，这般奇闻趣谈，在我们之间说说便好……

**Yao Guang:** Best not go spreading them around. If any of the Cosmicon Collective players find out, we'll never hear the end of their complaints about how "mountain-busting power levels are for noobs."

> **爻光：**可不要逢人就讲，若是传到《银河战力党》的玩家耳中，怕是要引来一番「爆山级战力仅是入门」的非议了。

**Evanescia:** I never expected a high-ranking Xianzhou General to be so well-versed in the gossip here on Planarcadia.

> **绯英：**真没想到，你这位高权重的仙舟将军，对乐园街头巷尾流行的谈资倒也了如指掌？

**Yao Guang:** Such is the way of a diviner. To enter a place is to observe its local custom.

> **爻光：**卜者行事，自当如此，入一地则遍察一地风物。

**Evanescia:** Same goes for us arbiters. Times change, and my babies have to change with 'em.

> **绯英：**我这裁决者也一样，时代变了，我的宝贝也要应时变化的。

**Evanescia:** Just picture it: a pretty girl in a school uniform, strutting through the streets with a giant sword slung over her shoulder? Yeah, right.

> **绯英：**想想看，堂堂学生装美少女，总不能整天扛着一把刀在大街上招摇过市吧？

**Yao Guang:** I see you are a law-abiding citizen, Miss Evanescia. Cute.

> **爻光：**绯英小姐遵纪守法，可爱呢。

**Yao Guang:** Now then, please begin your "measuring."

> **爻光：**那么，请开始你的「丈量」吧。

**Evanescia:** Weigh and sever this world, and engrave the laws of the cosmos — Spiritbranch of True Law!

> **绯英：**衡断此界，铭刻天规之尺刀——真律灵枝！

**Evanescia:** By the scale that adjudicates reality and illusion, truth and falsehood... measure for me all sins beneath the Phantasmoon that transgress the law!

> **绯英：**于此裁定虚实真伪的刻度，为吾衡量幻月之下一切逾越法规之罪！

**Evanescia:** What... is going on?

> **绯英：**这是…怎么回事？

**Evanescia:** It's telling me... Fulwish is not a Supplicant!

> **绯英：**它告诉我…满愿并非谒者！

**Evanescia:** If she's not a Supplicant, then there's no way to judge whether she's transgressed or not.

> **绯英：**既然不是谒者，要裁断逾矩，也就无从谈起。

**Yao Guang:** ...What do you mean?

> **爻光：**…什么意思？

**Yao Guang:** If Fulwish isn't a Supplicant, why would she openly declare that she's joining the Phantasmoon Games? And that mask back at the altar shaped like an Octomorph... Just who does it belong to?

> **爻光：**如果满愿不是谒者，那她为什么公然宣称自己会加入幻月游戏？秘庭神坛上那张形如「八足之蛸」的面具，究竟属于谁？

**Evanescia:** Behind that mask... countless Supplicants are wearing it. They're spread across all Seaf— No, all of Duomension City too...

> **绯英：**那张面具背后…有不可胜数的谒者佩戴着它，这些人眼下遍布整个海原…不，还有二维市——

**Evanescia:** Supplicants... They're everywhere!

> **绯英：**…到处都是谒者！

**Evanescia:** Not in the history of the Phantasmoon Games... have I ever seen a situation like this!

> **绯英：**这样的情况，自幻月游戏有史以来…我也从没见过！

**Sunday:** General, someone is approaching your position. It's Dan Heng, and...

> **星期日：**将军，有人在靠近你们。是丹恒，还有……

**Yao Guang:** Dan Heng, you've returned. And the one beside you...

> **爻光：**丹恒，你回来了。而你身边这位……

**Yao Guang:** Blade... Or perhaps I should call you by your true name, one of the Xianzhou's most wanted criminals: Yingxing.

> **爻光：**刃…也许我应该称你为仙舟重犯，应星才对。

**Yao Guang:** You've delivered yourself into my hands. Good.

> **爻光：**你来自投罗网，很好。

**Blade:** General of the Yuque... your vision remains confined within the laws of the Xianzhou.

> **刃：**玉阙的将军…你的眼界依旧只困在仙舟的法度之内。

**Dan Heng:** ...

> **丹恒：**……

**Blade:** I came here to kill our common enemy.

> **刃：**我是为杀死你我共同的敌人而来的。

**Yao Guang:** Our common enemy? You are the Xianzhou's enemy. Ever since you became a Stellaron Hunter, you have slain countless emissaries of judgment sent by the Xuling's Palace of Law to capture you. You even incited the artisans of the Zhuming to reforge your blade, entangling them in your crimes...

> **爻光：**你我共同的敌人？你就是仙舟的敌人。自你成为星核猎手后，杀死虚陵「枢律宫」派出拘捕你的诛罪使无算；又煽动朱明的匠作为你修补铸剑，受你牵累……

**Yao Guang:** But your most unforgivable crime... is daring to break into the Ten-Lords Commission restricted area on the Xianzhou Yuque with your accomplices. You destroyed the Ashen Stelewood, disturbed the slumber of our valiant heroes, and stole the secrets of the dead.

> **爻光：**而最不可饶恕的是，你竟敢与同伙强闯我玉阙仙舟十王司的禁地，破坏「归尘碑林」，惊扰阵亡英魂安眠,，窃取逝者秘辛。

**Yao Guang:** What kind of "vision" drove you to commit the countless crimes of your past? And what common enemy could possibly stand between you and me? By all means, enlighten me.

> **爻光：**是什么样的「眼界」令你犯下过去的累累罪行？你我之间，又有什么共同的敌人？我倒是愿闻其详。

**Ashveil:** Oh? While I was in seclusion all these years, you've clearly been up to quite a lot.

> **不死途：**嚯，我隐居的这些年，你小子可真没闲着啊？

**Ashveil:** If I'd known you were such a magnet for trouble back when you came to the Zhuming begging for a sword, I would've dragged you out of the forge and drafted you into my crew myself, tied up if necessary...

> **不死途：**早知你这么能惹事，当初来朱明求剑时，我用绑的也要把你从铸炼宫里拖走入伙……

**Yao Guang:** And this would be...?

> **爻光：**这位又是……？

**Ashveil:** A pleasure to meet you, General Yao Guang. It's truly touching that my friends on the Xianzhou have kept me in their thoughts for all these years.

> **不死途：**初次见面，爻光将军，有劳仙舟的朋友惦记我这么多年。

**Ashveil:** I never change my name, no matter where I roam. I am La Mancha.

> **不死途：**行不更名，坐不改姓，我就是拉曼查。

**Blade:** You're obviously using a fake name.

> **刃：**你明明改了假名。

**Yao Guang:** The head of the Galaxy Rangers... I didn't expect this trip to come with such a pleasant surprise.

> **爻光：**巡海游侠之首…没想到，这趟旅行还有意外之喜。

**Dan Heng:** General, I'm afraid old grudges will have to wait—

> **丹恒：**将军，旧日的恩怨恐怕得暂且搁置——

**Dan Heng:** What we need to discuss concerns the survival of this world, and that catastrophic calamity you foresaw in your divination.

> **丹恒：**我们要谈的事，关乎这个世界的存亡，也关乎你在占卜中预见的那场灭顶之灾。

**Blade:** I won't deny the crimes you've listed.

> **刃：**你列数的那些罪状，我不否认。

**Blade:** But I know full well that the Xianzhou cares nothing for the life or death of someone guilty like me. What you truly want...

> **刃：**但我很清楚，仙舟在意的并不是我这个有罪之身的死活，你们真正想要的……

**Blade:** ...is to exact a blood debt from the nemesis who has repeatedly awakened demonic stars, summoned abominations, and could not be slain even after a hundred deaths...

> **刃：**是向那个屡次唤醒妖星，召聚孽物，百杀不死的大敌索偿血债……

**Blade:** You wish to subdue the Emanator of the Abundance, Shuhu, once again... and even deny THEM rebirth for all eternity.

> **刃：**你们想要重新镇伏丰饶令使「倏忽」,乃至令祂永世不得超生。

**Blade:** And now, General, I have brought this opportunity before you.

> **刃：**而现在，将军，我把这个机会带到了你面前。

**Blade:** If you are dead set on condemning me to answer for my crimes, I will submit to your judgment and board the Navis Astrigera for the Xuling's inquisition.

> **刃：**若你一心想判罪雪恨，我就听从发落，乖乖随你登上天幢去虚陵受审。

**Blade:** But as I said, I did not come here today to play the savior. I came to stop the past from repeating itself, and... to prevent our common enemy from having their way.

> **刃：**但正如我说的，我今日来此，不为扮演救世主，而是为了旧事不必一再重演，还有…不让你我共同的敌人得逞。

**Blade:** General, if you do not want the fate of the Battle of the Fanghu from thirty years ago to fall at your feet then you must believe what I am about to say—

> **刃：**将军不想让三十年前「方壶血战」的命运落在眼前的世界，就应当相信我接下来说的话——

**Blade:** The "Happiness Surgery" promoted by the Smiles Society utilizes none other than Shuhu's cells, cells acquired from an unknown source.

> **刃：**幸研会推行的「幸福手术」所用的，正是不知何来的倏忽细胞。

**Yao Guang:** You mean... everyone who underwent the surgery...

> **爻光：**你的意思是，所有接受了手术的人……

**Blade:** ...have all become Supplicants. Once the Wishpower converges, the will of the flesh will awaken and turn every host into nourishment.

> **刃：**…都已成为谒者。只待愿力聚流，血肉的意志便会苏醒，将所有宿主化为养料。

**Yao Guang:** The one wearing the Octomorph Mask is not a single person. It is... an Emanator of the Abundance, dismembered and currently parasitizing the bodies of the masses here.

> **爻光：**佩戴「八足之蛸」假面的，并非一个人。而是已被肢解、正寄生于此地万民体内的…丰饶令使。

**Yao Guang:** Who on earth... could have brought something so terrifying to Planarcadia?

> **爻光：**究竟是谁…把如此可怕的东西带到了二相乐园？

Switching to Sparxie's POV...

Meanwhile, Sparxie is at the tavern, chatting it up and enjoying herself...

## 186　If We Spoke in Laughter　如果我们的语言是笑声

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域："World's End" Tavern　|　角色：Trailblazer; Sparxie; Giovanni; Zhongshan; Fulwish; Sparkle　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''A Superhero Caught in a Moral Dilemma'' completed

### Answer little raccoon's call

[Recap] Sparxie's intuition told her that Fulwish really did possess the crazy streak of a Masked Fool. In exchange for the Supplicant's Mask, Sparxie agreed to investigate Fulwish's identity as a Fool and headed off to ask Giovanni for information.

At the same time, in the tavern...

**Fool Fanning the Flames:** ...What innocence? We all saw it with our own eyes: You got your Supplicant's mask stolen and were strung up for a beating!

> **拱火的愚者：**…什么清白？我们大伙都是亲眼看见的：你被夺走了谒者的面具，吊起来打！

**Bystanding Fool:** Yeah, you even went out of your way to set up a livestream for yourself! Afraid the whole universe would miss your "heroic bearing", huh? A livestreamed public punishment! Hahahaha!

> **看热闹的愚者：**是啊，还特意给自己搞了个直播呢！生怕全宇宙错过你的英姿是吧？直播公开处刑！哈哈哈哈哈！

**Sparxie:** What do you mean, "stolen"!? Can a Supplicant's affairs really be considered "stealing"? That's called... tactical emotional engagement! A tactic AND an art! Don't you get it?

> **火花：**什么「夺走」！谒者的事儿，能算「夺走」吗？那叫…战术性情感互动，是战术也是艺术，懂不懂？

**Sparxie:** Yoo-hoo! If it isn't the Astral Express's famous li'l raccoon~ Did you miss my pranks?

> **火花：**哟吼，这不是星穹列车盛名远播的小浣熊嘛~想念火花花的恶作剧了？

**(Trailblazer):** Did you get the invitation for the Supplicant debate at Fulwish TV?

> **开拓者：**关于满愿电视台的谒者辩论邀请,你收到了吗？

**Sparxie:** My oh my, talk about bad timing~ My stream got banned, and the leader of the haters confiscated my Supplicant mask. As for the debate, I'm afraid the spirit is willing, but the flesh is weak...

> **火花：**哎呀呀，真不巧~本主播的直播间被封，谒者假面也被黑粉头子没收了，这辩论大会啊，我是有心无力——

**Sparxie:** But seeing as you called, you clearly still have a shred of conscience left in you. Are you planning to give me a chance to haggle?

> **火花：**不过你既然打来了电话，可见你还是有二两良心在身上的，是不是打算给我个讨价还价的机会？

**(Trailblazer):** Answer me this question first: Is Fulwish really a Masked Fool?

> **开拓者：**先回答我的问题：满愿，真的是假面愚者吗？

**Sparxie:** The Tavern doesn't exactly register every Fool's name and address...

**Sparxie:** But having watched her stream, I believe she is. As for why... call it a kindred intuition. And...

> **火花：**「酒馆」可不会让每个愚者登记姓名住址……

**Sparxie:** Just listen to her slogan: "Healing Smiles; Healing Arcadias"... That level of crazy? She's a certified Fool.

> **火花：**不过嘛，看了她的直播，我相信她是。要说理由嘛…同类的直觉,还有——听听她的口号：「治愈微笑，治愈乐园」…疯得这么厉害，如假包换的愚者。

**Sparxie:** Most Masked Fools make a huge racket when they approach you. That "Hey look, I'm about to stir up some trouble" vibe is impossible to hide.

> **火花：**虽然大部分假面愚者靠近你的时候都咋咋呼呼的，那股子「快看」我要搞事了」的劲头藏也藏不住。

**Sparxie:** But Fulwish is clearly the exception. Her brand of crazy is calm and sophisticated.

> **火花：**但，满愿显然是个例外，她疯得很冷静、很高级。

**Sparxie:** Look at that haute couture getup she's wearing, it's worth ten times a normal person's salary. Who'd have guessed a career woman with state levels of wealth who is always preaching social welfare would be a Masked Fool? Simply the most exquisite disguise!

> **火花：**看看她那身抵得上普通人十年薪水的高级定制行头，谁会想到一个富可敌国、整日呼吁社会公益事业的女强人会是个假面愚者？简直是最绝妙的伪装！

**(Trailblazer):** I'd like to ask you to look into her background for me.

> **开拓者：**我想拜托你，帮我查查她的底细。

**Sparxie:** Now that's going too far! You actually want me to betray another Masked Fool...!?

> **火花：**太过分了！你居然要我背叛另一个假面愚者……？！

**(Trailblazer):** Give me the intel on Fulwish, and I'll return the mask to you.

> **开拓者：**给我满愿的情报，我会把面具还给你。

**Sparxie:** Hahaha! Betrayal itself, that's the finest reward of all!

> **火花：**哈哈哈！背叛，这活儿本身，就是最好的报酬啦！

**Sparxie:** Deal! Leave it to me. I'll contact you as soon as I get the intel.

> **火花：**成交！包在我身上了，我一拿到情报就联系你。

**Sparxie:** Fulwish... Is there really someone like that among the Masked Fools?

> **火花：**满愿…假面愚者里真有这号人嘛？

**Sparxie:** How come I'd never heard of her?

> **火花：**我怎么听都没听过？

**Sparxie:** Maybe we should ask Giovanni about her. He's got connections everywhere, after all.

> **火花：**也许该从乔瓦尼那儿打听打听，毕竟他人脉广。

Not long after, Sparxie arrived at Giovanni's location.

*(Unlock Achievement Arbiter's Verdict)*

### Head to the storage area and find Giovanni

> *update*

[Recap] There's no record of Fulwish in the storage area's registry. But Giovanni tipped Sparxie off: If Fulwish stepped into the tavern after the Bloodstained Games, they could try flipping through the medical records at "Fatal Joke."

*(Talk to Giovanni)*

**Sparxie:** Woo-hoo, Mr. Giovanni! Long time no see!

> **火花：**哟吼！乔瓦尼先生——！好久不见了！

**Giovanni:** Miss Spar... Sparxie. You just looted Sparkle's entire inventory right from under my nose. How can you say "long time no see"?

> **乔瓦尼：**花…火花小姐。你才在我的眼皮子底下洗劫了花火的库存道具，怎么能说「好久不见」？

**Giovanni:** Please, spare me. Just give me a straight answer: Whose collection are you planning to steal this time?

### Read Fulwish's medical records

[Recap] Sparxie found Fulwish's medical records, which detailed a symptom known as "Happiness Syntax." Zhongshan explained that upon receiving the Fool's Mask at the age of sixteen, Fulwish wanted to smash it to pieces. Despite using all her strength, she had only managed to leave a single crack. The power that erupted when the mask cracked also left a "rift" in the wall, and every Fool who had ever tried to step inside has gone mad. Undeterred, Sparxie insisted on entering the "rift" to explore.

**Sparxie:** Fulwish... Fulwish...

**Sparxie:** Ah, found it!

(Quick read)

Medical Record: Fulwish

**Name:** Fulwish Sex: Female Homeland: Planarcadia Species: Humanoid, Category: Ahatopian Subspecies Department: Cranial Neurology Department Chief Complaint: After experiencing significant psychological trauma, the patient developed an unknown disorder in the language center. Specific symptoms include an inability to describe negative feedback received by the self, and an inability to actively express the pain perceived by the self. Meanwhile, the patient exhibits hallucinatory symptoms, described as the ability to sense the inner desires of others. Past Medical History: The patient is in good health with no relevant medical history or family history of hereditary diseases. Patient previously suffered from post-traumatic stress disorder. Examination Report: Electroencephalogram, cerebral lobe structural imaging, and modeling analysis indicate no foreign bodies or visible damage to the patient's brain. Mild non-pathological tissue growth detected at the Synesthesia Beacon implant site. The cause remains unknown. Following a localized Wishpower detection (approved by the Department of Aberration Defense), low-frequency undetectable imagery appeared in the anterior cingulate cortex, but hasn't significantly affected the cranial nerves yet. Its correlation with the patient's condition remains unverified, and the possibility of psychologically induced language disruption can't be ruled out. Testing confirms the Patient can accurately describe the desires of others. Simultaneously, as the Patient verbalizes these descriptions, the empathetic subject experiences intense hallucinations and loses all rationality. The mechanism behind this remains unclear, though it appears to fall under hypnotic or telepathic phenomena. For now, we'll designate the patient's unusual condition as "Happiness Syntax." Prescription: Aberration-Interference Sustained-Release Tablets Type II. Take 2 tablets daily, one in the morning and one in the evening, 30 minutes after meals. Dosage will be adjusted based on disease progression and follow-up evaluations. Treatment: Schedule a visit to the clinic upon feeling unwell.

Pre-Op Psychological Assessment Record

Psychological consultation for members experiencing pre-op anxiety. The session has been archived per standard protocol. (Patient information has been anonymized for confidentiality.) The consultation record is as follows: Fulwish: Praise the Master of Elation! Is everyone having a happy day today? Member A: I... I'm so happy. (uneasy, restless) Fulwish: No, you're not happy. If you were, you wouldn't be here right now. Today I'm not speaking as the president of the Smiles Society. Think of me as just a normal friend, someone you can open up to about what's troubling you. Member A: Lady... Fulwish... I'll admit, if it weren't for everyone at the Smiles Society, on the day I got laid off, I probably would've... But with your encouragement, I found a new job and even gained a new family. I think I should give it one more shot and try to find happiness through my own strength, instead of jumping straight into the surgery. Fulwish: You're absolutely right. The Society isn't the IPC. We'd never force our members to do anything. Today's just a friendly chat between friends, so relax. Let's just talk about life, shall we? How's work been lately? Getting along with your coworkers? Is everyone happy? Member A: ...Yeah, everyone's so happy, bursting with energy every single day, and it's all thanks to your help. But sometimes, I feel like we're from two different worlds. Whenever I'm exhausted and just want to sigh, I take one look at them and... it's like I'm doing something I'm not allowed to do. Fulwish: I went through the same thing in the days right after the Bloodstained Games ended. Everyone was so kind, but the more help I accepted, the guiltier I felt. I was scared that if they saw even a hint of unease on my face, it would hurt them too. I was scared that if I cried, it would make them sad. But that only made me lonelier, made me think that misfortune itself was a kind of original sin. Member A: Yeah, I totally get that! It's like this vibe hanging over all of Planarcadia. Whether it's at the company, out on the streets, or online... seeing everyone laughing and having a blast always makes me feel like I should join in, but... Fulwish: Don't blame yourself. Pursuing happiness is human nature. It's precisely because you aren't happy enough that you yearn for that world of happiness, and you've mistaken that yearning for alienation. Picture this: everyone around you is laughing, but you're the only one in tears. Your wife and kids are happily chatting away nearby, while all you can do is heave a heavy sigh. Do you really want to turn your back on them? Or would you rather bask in that atmosphere of happiness, stand by their side, and share a laugh? Happiness is a divine gift, not something to be ashamed of. When it comes down to it, do you truly resist the Happiness Surgery? You simply haven't recognized your true self yet. Tell me, what is it you really want? Member A: I want to... join them. I want to be happy.
