> **星期日：**他们用以操纵人心的，何尝不是「恐惧与美梦」——即使没有谒者的身份，我也不会袖手旁观。

**Ashveil:** So, she doesn't stand a chance in the debate then? She's ticked off so many heavy hitters... I really thought she'd have some smarter tricks up her sleeve.

> **不死途：**这么说，在辩论里她不是没戏唱了？惹上这么多硬茬，还以为有多高明的手段。

- ▸ What if she is really good at debate?

> ▸ 万一她辩论水平很高呢？

- ▸ When the time comes, what should I say?

> ▸ 到时候，我该说些什么？

**Ashveil:** When it comes down to it, it's just like the Phantasmoon Games, people love surprises, so over-preparing is pointless. I've met plenty of big shots, but nobody has a knack for grabbing attention like you do. Use that to your advantage and play things by ear.

> **不死途：**说到底，还是和幻月游戏一样，人们都期待惊喜，准备太多反而没用。我见过不少大人物，但吸引眼球这块，没人有你这样的天赋——好好利用，见招拆招。

**Sunday:** Precisely because we were once adversaries, I don't think (Trailblazer) has anything to worry about.

> **星期日：**正因我们曾是敌手，在我看来，开拓者完全不需要担心。

**Ashveil:** And I'll be joining in too, anyway. Just shoot me a glance if you need me, and I'll back you up.

> **不死途：**反正我也会一起参加——需要的时候，给个眼神就行，我会帮你说话的。

- ▸ Any advice, Himeko?

> ▸ 姬子的建议呢？

- ▸ I'm going to win.

> ▸ 我一定会赢的。

**Himeko:** Don't put too much pressure on yourself. Those obsessed with winning... are usually the ones who lose.

> **姬子：**别给自己太多压力——执着于胜利的人，往往都会失败。

**Himeko:** When I left Planarcadia, someone once said this to me:

> **姬子：**离开二相乐园时，曾有人这样对我说——

**Himeko:** "When moving forward, never ask too many questions. The moment we stop to think, doubt seeps in, fear of becoming a laughingstock, or of being rejected. It sounds absurd, yet that's how people are."

> **姬子：**「前进时绝不要多问原因，一旦停下来思考，我们的心灵就会被怀疑吞没——害怕成为笑柄，又或遭人拒绝。听来荒谬，但人们总会如此。」

**Himeko:** Don't let the word "debate" lead you astray. Just be yourself, as you always have been. When it comes to you, my faith is unwavering.

> **姬子：**别被「辩论」这个词带进误区，就像过去那样，做你自己就好——而对于你，我永远都会抱有信心。

**Fulwish:** Hello viewers... I regret that we must meet under these circumstances.

> **满愿：**各位观众，很遗憾以这种形式和你们见面。

**Fulwish:** This is an emergency broadcast: A Supplicant is attacking Seafeld City with the power... of... the Harmony....

**Ashveil:** ...What kind of commotion is this?

> **不死途：**…这又是什么动静？

### Listen to what's on the radio

> *Sounds like someone's talking on the broadcast. Some kind of mystery show? But seriously, for a TV station HQ, these speakers have terrible audio quality.*

*(Idle talk of the employees north)*

The IPC really is nothing but trouble!

*(Walk towards the employees north or east)*

**Ashveil:** Weird. What are they—

> **不死途：**怪了，他们这是……

*(Idle talk of the employees)Please add location.*

So they're the ones who did that to the Phantasmoon Games...

Master of Elation above...

*(Idle talk of the employees west)*

So it really is them...

*(Walk towards the employees west or south)*

**Himeko:** They look a bit scared. Could it be related to the broadcast just now?

> **姬子：**他们看起来有些害怕，难道和刚才的播报有关？

*(Idle talk of the employees south)*

Don't, don't look at me...

He... He's the...

*(Idle talk of the employees east)*

I never should've been so polite...

Where's the magical girl? Hurry up, call her over...

[Recap] Fulwish presented the public with one-sided facts skewed against you, called off the public debate, and claimed that the power causing the flesh of the Abundance to become infectious stemmed from the Harmony.

*(Approach mission target)*

**Ashveil:** What? Is Fulwish TV up to their old tricks again?

> **不死途：**怎么？满愿电视台又想玩花样了？

**Fulwish:** Hello viewers... I regret that we must meet under these circumstances.

> **满愿：**各位观众，很遗憾以这种形式和你们见面。

**Fulwish:** As you all know, to prevent a repeat of the Bloodstained Games, I organized a televised debate. I hoped the dispute between Supplicants would stop at words, and the right to decide the winner would be shared by everyone.

> **满愿：**众所周知，为了不让血涂游戏重演，我筹备了一场电视辩论——我希望谒者间的争端止于言语，也将决定胜者的权利交还给每一个人。

**Fulwish:** I was confident in my victory. I was ready to fulfill my promise, to build an arcadia free of worry, where the only thing people need do is laugh.

> **满愿：**我很有信心取得胜利，完成自己曾经的诺言——创造一个没有烦恼，人人只需欢笑的乐园。

**Fulwish:** But now, I must cancel the debate and reveal the truth to the public. For what these outsiders seek to tarnish is more than just the Phantasmoon Games.

> **满愿：**可现在，我却必须取消辩论，将真相公之于众。因为这些外来者想要玷污的，不仅仅是幻月游戏。

**Fulwish:** I implore all citizens: Do not panic at the footage to follow. We are facing an unprecedented danger, but this is not a time for despair.

> **满愿：**我恳请各位民众，不要为以下画面感到恐慌：我们正身处前所未见的危险中，但还远远不到绝望的时候。

**Fulwish:** Our reporter in Seafeld City captured this scene. This man should be no stranger to you. His name is Dan Heng, a member of the Astral Express and a renowned hero.

> **满愿：**在海原市，本台记者拍到了这样一幕。这名男性，各位应该不陌生——他名为丹恒，是星穹列车的一员，赫赫有名的英雄。

**Fulwish:** And yet, accompanying him is none other than the Xianzhou's wanted criminal, the Stellaron Hunter, Blade. Rumor has it he was once tainted by the flesh of the Abundance, and could go berserk at any moment.

> **满愿：**可与他同行的，却是仙舟的通缉犯，星核猎手刃——据说，他曾被丰饶的血肉侵染，随时可能凶性大发。

**Fulwish:** Back then, I trusted the Astral Express, figuring this was just a simple capture operation...

> **满愿：**那时，我相信星穹列车，猜测这只是一次抓捕行动……

**Fulwish:** But a short while ago, I found members of the Smiles Society infected with the flesh and blood of the Abundance! Only then did I realize what sort of conspiracy I was being dragged into...

> **满愿：**可就在不久前，幸福研究会中，发现了同样被丰饶血肉感染的会员！我这才意识到，自己正被卷入怎样的阴谋——

**Fulwish:** Aside from (Trailblazer), there's another Supplicant aboard the Astral Express who used an alias not too long ago to infiltrate the Smiles Society.

> **满愿：**除了开拓者，星穹列车上还有另一位谒者。不久前，他曾化名潜入幸福研究会。

**Fulwish:** But his true identity reveals him to be an interstellar fugitive, a member of The Family... Sunday. And by sheer coincidence, he traveled with the IPC-backed Ashveil, arriving at the debate venue together.

> **满愿：**而他的真正身份，也是一名星际通缉犯，家族成员，星期日。巧合的是，他偏偏又与受公司支持的不死途同行，一同来到了辩论现场。

**Fulwish:** Ladies and gentlemen, I won't summarize this, lest my own views mislead you. I will only present the indisputable facts for your judgment. May the Master of Elation bless us all.

> **满愿：**各位观众，我不会对此进行总结，以免自己的观点产生误导——我只会展示确凿无疑的事实，供你们进行判断，愿欢愉之主庇佑我们每一个人。

**Ashveil:** So that's the setup? Such blatant manipulation won't fool anyone for long.

> **不死途：**原来在这等着呢？这么明显的拨弄是非，骗不了人们多久。

**Himeko:** No... Everyone, we'd better get out of here.

> **姬子：**不…各位，我们还是先离开这里为好。

**Ashveil:** ...What is it?

> **不死途：**…怎么了？

**Himeko:** Maybe when we arrived at the TV station, the debate wasn't just starting...

> **姬子：**也许，我们来到电视台时，辩论不是才开始……

**Himeko:** ...but had already finished.

> **姬子：**…而是已经结束了。

**Fulwish:** This... Wait!? How could...

> **满愿：**这…等等？！怎么会……

**Fulwish:** Viewers, we interrupt with an emergency announcement: The Department of Aberration Defense has confirmed that the power causing the flesh of the Abundance to become infectious...

> **满愿：**各位观众，插播一条紧急通知：经异常防御部确认，让丰饶血肉开始出现传染性的力量……

**Fulwish:** ...originates from the Harmony.

> **满愿：**…源自「同谐」。

[Recap] Across Seafeld City's streets, large numbers of civilians had transformed into Joy Ascendants, triggering severe unrest. Evanescia drew the "Spiritbranch of True Law" and attempted to bring the situation under control. "It is time to fulfill my duty."

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Poofy:** A Supplicant is attacking Seafeld City with the power of the Harmony...

**Poofy:** Please... dear... citizens...

**Evanescia:** Enough small talk...

**Evanescia:** It is time to fulfill my duty.

*(Cutscene ends)*

(Begin Postmodern Imagenesis)

## 190　Postmodern Imagenesis　幻造化的后现代

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Seafeld City　|　角色：Trailblazer; Sunday; Himeko; Ashveil; Poofy; Snowie; Pearl; Yao Guang; Evanescia

### Stop the Joy Ascendants from wreaking havoc on the city

> *Tsk, in my imagination, this was supposed to be way more of a teenage girl comic-style comeback. Those bigwigs sitting in their fancy top-floor offices sure know how to make things difficult for people. But no matter who tries to ruin the "Game"... I won't allow it.*

*(Begin battle against Joy Ascendant ×1)*

**Smiling Flesh:** Let us... become one, and share in boundless Elation!

(After Joy Ascendant splits for the first time)

**Smiling Flesh:** One of me, one dose of Happiness... Two of me, that's double the bliss!

*(Battle ends)*

[Recap] Yao Guang questioned if Fulwish's atrocities violated the rules, but Evanescia brushed them off as a routine calamity in the millennia-old Phantasmoon Games. As long as the Supplicant could absorb Wishpower to replenish the Phantasmoon, anything goes in the game.

**Smiling Flesh:** Pain? What flavor of candy is that? I want more! Hahaha...

**Yao Guang:** Their senses have become completely distorted.

**Smiling Flesh:** Come closer... Just... a little more...

**Yao Guang:** Is he... dead?

**Evanescia:** No, the flesh is regenerating. It won't be long before he gets back up, no matter how many times he is killed.

**Yao Guang:** In that case, he is certainly worthy of the "Great Weal" fortune hanging over his head.

**Yao Guang:** So this is Fulwish's goal? To twist citizens implanted with Shuhu's cells into giggling abominations and wage war on every Supplicant? It's horrifying, to be sure. But...

**Yao Guang:** Fulwish is a mere mortal. How exactly did she do it?

**Evanescia:** General, please do not call them that. These people are nothing like the Denizens of Abundance you're familiar with.

**Evanescia:** They... are just normal people of Seafeld City, struggling with the grind of daily survival. They don't have the strength that you and I possess, nor have they ever seen the world outside of Planarcadia.

**Evanescia:** They just want to stop and catch their breath... even if that breath is poison coughed up by Fulwish.

**Yao Guang:** You're right. They deserved sanctuary. But right now, there's another group of people who need our protection.

**Yao Guang:** Since we have the power, let's do something, whether for the survivors or the victims.

**Evanescia:** ...

**Yao Guang:** You were the referee of the Phantasmoon Games. Even if you were forced off the field by underhanded means, surely you retain some right of arbitration?

**Yao Guang:** Now the land is overrun by walking corpses and the people are plunged into misery! Are you telling me the Supplicant who committed such atrocities still hasn't committed a foul!?

**Evanescia:** This is merely a common occurrence in a game that has lasted thousands of years: Calamity.

**Evanescia:** So long as the Supplicant can draw Wishpower to fill the Phantasmoon, the game knows no taboos.

**Evanescia:** The Master of Elation allows Supplicants to enact the utmost benevolence, beauty, truth, greed, and absurdity, even the utmost evil.

**Yao Guang:** Then where exactly do the boundaries of the Phantasmoon Games lie?

**Evanescia:** Since when do Aha's games ever have boundaries?

**Evanescia:** You don't understand, General.

**Yao Guang:** Then make me understand.

**Evanescia:** ...As you wish.

[Recap] Evanescia stated that the Phantasmoon Games had only one taboo: the game must go on, endlessly. She was able to serve as the arbitrator because she possessed the power to eradicate those who transgress.

**Evanescia:** I wonder if you've heard of the tragedy of the Bloodstained Games from fifteen years ago, General?

**Yao Guang:** Compared to the catastrophic history of the Phantasmoon Games, that was likely nothing more than an insignificant interlude, no?

**Evanescia:** Countless times, I wanted to reach out and touch those muted hearts to make them beat again.

**Evanescia:** But I can't save everyone. It's always been this way.

**Evanescia:** The Phantasmoon Games have only one taboo, and there is only one rule I strictly abide by—

**Evanescia:** The games must proceed, never-ending just as the sun and moon rise and fall. Those who defy this law shall be banished, executed, and suffer unceasing torment.

**Evanescia:** General Yao Guang, have you ever wondered: Why am I the arbitrator?

**Yao Guang:** Simply because you possess the power to eradicate those who transgress.

[Recap] Evanescia recounted a somewhat dubious mythological tale: Aha once transplanted a "severed branch" blessed by the Abundance here, and in doing so, imprisoned the most terrifying entity within the most ridiculous game. Throughout the cycles of the Phantasmoon Games, the "plucked branch" gradually came to understand the Elation, and thus Evanescia was born. Over the past millennium, Evanescia had called upon the power of the Abundance three times to avert a crisis, each time leaving behind irreversible consequences. Now, Evanescia was afraid to use this power again.

**Evanescia:** Eons ago, in the Master of Immortality's mysterious garden, flora flourished with such intensity that they suffocated all other life. A meddler quietly infiltrated this place, plucking the most enchanting and luxuriant branch.

**Evanescia:** The severed branch screamed in protest. For before she was gifted with eternal life, she was a divine envoy leading her kin in search of the elixir of life. She was a beast of blind love and hatred, a sword dancer who devoured the Crimson Moon... so proud was she, never having known how to yield.

**Evanescia:** Save for the blazing Phoenix of the Xianzhou, she has never been defeated by another. How, then, could she tolerate... a meddler defiling her fragrance?

**Yao Guang:** Are you speaking of myths... or true history?

**Evanescia:** When the past is as distant as the afterglow of the stars, does it really matter whether it is myth or history ?

**Evanescia:** That meddler transplanted her to another world. And through this method, THEY imprisoned the most terrifying of horrors within the most ridiculous of games.

**Yao Guang:** You mean... you yourself are that "most terrifying thing"?

**Evanescia:** You've misunderstood... or perhaps not.

**Evanescia:** The meddlesome god promised to enlighten her, to teach her to know true joy, and in the repeated cycles of the Phantasmoon Games, the meddler did not break THEIR promise.

**Evanescia:** She once witnessed raging flames boil the seas dry, saw civilizations rise from dust and wither back into it. She gazed upon the purest souls burning away their brilliance, and stood silent as supreme evil reveled in blood upon the earth.

**Evanescia:** Finally, amidst the love and hate of the insignificant, she gradually glimpsed the essence of fleeting life: The meaning of Elation.

**Evanescia:** Finally, she became me.

**Yao Guang:** So, you're afraid of waking her... afraid of becoming her once more.

**Evanescia:** Over the past thousand years, I have called upon her power three times to avert catastrophe. Each time, it left an irreparable scar upon the land.

**Yao Guang:** This is the Abundance... Healing and torture, co-existing as one.

[Recap] Yao Guang encouraged Evanescia to exercise her arbitration authority, but that required a rule-breaker. Since there were currently no rule-breaking Supplicants, Evanescia decided to break the rules herself, revealing the true purpose of this Aeonic game to Yao Guang.

**Evanescia:** Even knowing everything, would you still have me wear that fox mask and exercise the authority of arbitration?

**Yao Guang:** Miss Evanescia, what you are terrified of is not this fox mask.

**Yao Guang:** Quite the opposite. "Evanescia" is the mask you wear: What you are terrified of is your true self.

**Yao Guang:** I, too, was once terrified of my mask.

**Evanescia:** Your mask?

**Yao Guang:** A mask known as Arbiter-General.

**Yao Guang:** Whenever the tides of war demand it, I have no choice but to don it, to become that "Slaughtering General": coldly watching blood and tears be shed, plotting and divining beneath the astral charts, even accepting the scorn of bereaved orphans, all to ensure the Lux Arrow is loosed.

**Evanescia:** I don't understand. As a General of the Xianzhou, why have you urged me all this time... urged this creation of the Abundance to reveal its true form...?

**Yao Guang:** It's laughable, really. I arrived in Planarcadia for the sole purpose of involving the Master of Elation in my divination, seeking to rewrite a fate already cast in stone.

**Yao Guang:** But to steer the orbits of the stars is no easy feat. Whether it be the Lux Arrow that topples the sky, or the games of the Elation... It's difficult to grasp even if you go all out.

**Yao Guang:** From the instant the Phantasmoon's declaration increased the stakes, my divination has spiraled out of control. My fate shifts ceaselessly between adversity and prosperity, life and death, becoming impossible to fathom.

**Yao Guang:** With the Aeons in play, divination is futile. The only thing you can ever put trust in, is people. Just as I once believed my teacher could successfully fulfill their fate—

**Yao Guang:** I really believe in you too, Miss Evanescia.

**Evanescia:** ...Has anyone ever told you you're quite the gambler? Daring to push your chips onto an unknown quantity like me... in a game where the Aeons THEMSELVES have taken a seat at the table?

**Yao Guang:** When we first met, I could not gain insight into the weal or woe of your fate, yet I was willing to trust you. I believe you are also the key to rewriting destiny in this game whose ending no one can foresee.

**Evanescia:** Why?

**Yao Guang:** Miss Evanescia, the Spiritus that watches over me is named the "Kālacakra Skyplume." It is a symbol of The Hunt's foresight, verdict, and self-discipline.

**Yao Guang:** Why does the decisive god of the Skybow bear such a visage? After much contemplation, I finally understood.

**Yao Guang:** If the value of discipline was understood, the creations of the Abundance need not be abominations. And without temperance, the consequences of The Hunt are no different from Destruction.

**Yao Guang:** I am not some gambler, nor am I an ant beneath the gaze of the Aeons...

**Yao Guang:** I was the one who offered up a piece of my own insignificant fate, forging THEIR trajectories.

**Evanescia:** ...

**Yao Guang:** The situation outside is volatile, we don't have a second to spare for idle chatter. Miss Evanescia, I've given you my answer... and I'd like to hear yours in return.

**Yao Guang:** When you realized Fulwish was not a Supplicant and you could not pass judgment, why did I see hesitation and doubt in your eyes rather than your usual indifference?

**Yao Guang:** I know you already have a game changer in mind. You're just not ready to use it yet.

**Yao Guang:** Please, tell me your solution.

**Evanescia:** As you wish. To exercise the right of arbitration, irrefutable proof is required, proof that a Supplicant has indeed broken the rules and disrupted the order of the Phantasmoon Games.

**Evanescia:** Yet surveying the board, I see not a single transgression. Since there are no sinners... then I shall become the "sinner".

**Evanescia:** General Yao Guang, you once asked me: Where do the boundaries of the Phantasmoon Games lie? Well here's my answer: The boundaries aren't on the outside, but at the core.

**Evanescia:** Now, I shall transgress the taboo and divulge to you the true purpose of this Aeonic game—

**Evanescia:** Through this method, the Master of Elation imprisoned the most terrifying of horrors within the most ridiculous of games.

**Evanescia:** The terror slumbering at the heart of Planarcadia is:

**Evanescia:**████████████████████

Returning to the Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

## 191　Metaphors We Live By　我们赖以生存的隐喻

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Pearluxe Tower　|　角色：Silver Wolf; Pearl; Kafka; Feld　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''A Resounding Gong or a Clashing Cymbal'' completed

### Talk with Pearl about what just happened

> *The claws of Abundance have reached into Planarcadia. Looks like the Elation's little game needs to hit the pause button.*

[Recap] Pearl met with Silver Wolf, wanting to see how the "script" would unfold, but Silver Wolf refused. Pearl granted Silver Wolf unrestricted network privileges, hoping she would investigate the truth behind the "Joy Ascendant." In exchange, Pearl would turn a blind eye to the Stellaron Hunters' suspicious activities for this duration.

**Agent Feld:** "...According to statistics, approximately 200,000 individuals fell ill simultaneously and were transformed into Denizens of Abundance."

> **菲尔德专员：**「目前，病变仍在乐园全域扩散：载具城、歌坛市无一幸免……」

**Pearl:** The affected staff were quarantined immediately. Pearluxe Tower is a valuable safe zone.

**Silver Wolf:** If you'd listened to me and thrown every suspect in prison, we wouldn't be in this mess.

**Silver Wolf:** So, why'd you call me here?

**Pearl:** It's an emergency. I want to see how the "script" plays out.

**Silver Wolf:** Huh?

**Pearl:** No one could have guessed an Emanator of the Abundance would return by hijacking others' bodies. Everyone is hesitant to act for fear of collateral damage.

**Pearl:** But since he is here as well... Deduction: This crisis was within your calculations all along.

**Silver Wolf:** Half right. Shuhu is the reason Blade made the trip. As for the outcome...

**Silver Wolf:** Tell you what, I'll show you the "script." It's too wordy, and I can't be bothered to paraphrase.

**Silver Wolf:** Here, sending you a copy.

**Pearl:** Hah...

**Pearl:** For the sake of our business partnership, save this virus copy for someone else, hacker.

**Silver Wolf:** The feeling is mutual. Seems you don't trust me much either.

**Pearl:** I told you, I've done my research on you... or to be precise, on all of you. Destiny's Slave may be able to foresee the future and pen "scripts" for the Stellaron Hunters, but in the face of Aeon intervention, his "vision" is powerless.

**Pearl:** However, the IPC has its own methods.

**Silver Wolf:** Oh?

**Pearl:** I'll grant you a privilege. Starting now, user "Silver Wolf" has unrestricted access to the arcadian internet.

**Silver Wolf:** I could've done that myself.

**Pearl:** With the precondition that I agree.

[Recap] Kafka sent a transmission to Silver Wolf, but it was 23 seconds later than the "script" had dictated. Elio asked Kafka to pass along a message to Silver Wolf: Don't lose to "Aha."

**Pearl:** I want you to hack into the internet, trace the source of the Joy Ascendant contagion, and figure out the catalyst and transmission vectors.

**Pearl:** Suppressing the surface commotion is a simple task. But if the root cause isn't addressed, the chaos can never be truly eradicated. In exchange, I'll turn a blind eye to the Stellaron Hunters' suspicious activities for the time being.

**Silver Wolf:** Heh. So this is what it means to see how the "script" plays out.

**Silver Wolf:** Maybe you should keep it wide open. One eye might not be enough.

**Pearl:** Let's start the investigation with the sick ones inside the building.

**Silver Wolf:** I thought this was supposed to be a Safe Zone?

**Pearl:** "Safe" means that I monitor everyone's every move, including yours...

**Pearl:** ...and hers.

**Kafka:** Judging by the time, I should be right on cue?

**Silver Wolf:** Not exactly "right on." You missed your cue. You should've made your entrance four lines ago.

**Pearl:** Four lines back? Oh... "One eye might not be enough." You were laying the groundwork for her entrance.

**Pearl:** Margin of error: 23 seconds. Coordination needs improvement.

**Kafka:** My bad, I lost track of time. What about you? Did the operation go smoothly?

**Silver Wolf:** It's a long story. I thought it was a Battle Royale, but it turned into a Zombie Crisis. I think it should've been you instead.

**Silver Wolf:** I heard Elio gave you a long vacation? Whereabouts?

**Kafka:** Let's call it a business trip. Ten days in Penacony with a packed schedule.

**Kafka:** Take today, for instance. I needed to arrive at Aideen Park on time, order two drinks, give the cash-spewer three knocks, and watch it spit out 650,000 credits...

**Kafka:** And then, I was supposed to get on the line and say hello to you.

**Silver Wolf:** 23 seconds late. Not very punctual.

**Kafka:** I don't mind. After all, even a late warning might just save you and Bladie's lives.

**Kafka:** Elio asked me to pass along a message—

**Kafka:** Don't lose to "Aha."

**Silver Wolf:** That's it? Anything else you wanna say?

**Kafka:** Hope you enjoy yourself!

**Pearl:** What secret messages have you been sending?

## 193　The Generative Grammar of Liquid Modernity　液态现代性的生成语法

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Seafeld TV Tower　|　角色：Trailblazer; Himeko; Sunday; Ashveil; Poofy; Snowie; Dan Heng; Blade; Fulwish; Pearl; Silver Wolf; Silver Wolf LV.999; Asat Pramad; Sparxie; Terminus; Furbulitzer　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Metaphors We Live By'' completed

> *To think we'd get slandered as accomplices of the IPC, when the real mastermind is someone else entirely. Besides, the IPC really is innocent this time. For Skott's sake, and for the Express Crew's reputation, you have to show the audience the truth behind this incident. As president of Furbobo Press, you have a duty to protect the three legendary pillars of journalism.*

### Find a way to leave here

> *The elevator's out of commission, and the stairs are blocked off. Gotta find a way around them and keep moving.*

[Recap] The broadcast claimed that the IPC was the true culprit behind the Herald of Death incident, and that the Astral Express was turning a blind eye. Public attitude toward the Express Crew plummeted to an all-time low. To turn the tide, you must expose Fulwish's lies.

**Broadcast:** "... Just moments ago, our reporter successfully exposed the Herald of Death scandal... and paid for it with their life!"

> **广播：**「…就在刚才，我台记者以生命为代价，成功揭发了告死魔事件！」

**Broadcast:** "The IPC used a monster named Shuhu to create an infectious virus."

> **广播：**「星际和平公司利用名为倏忽的怪物，制造出了一种带感染性的病毒。」

**Broadcast:** "Massive numbers of civilians have been transformed into the walking dead! Your family and friends could all fall victim!"

> **广播：**「大量平民被转化为行尸走肉，您的亲人、朋友都可能成为受害者！」

**Broadcast:** "As a local Supplicant, the Director Fulwish will fight to the bitter end at Seafeld TV Tower! Please lock your doors and windows and stay safe! Fulwish TV wishes you safe passage through this crisis!"

> **广播：**「身为本土谒者，满愿台长会在海原电视塔战斗到底！请关好门窗，注意安全！满愿电视台，祝您平安度过本次危机！

**Ashveil:** Listen to that. So we're full-blown villains now?

> **不死途：**这话说的，我们彻底成恶人了？

**Sunday:** As for the remaining Supplicants...

> **星期日：**剩下的谒者……

**Ashveil:** I can't reach them. We'll just have to take things one step at a time.

> **不死途：**联系不上。走一步看一步吧。

**Sunday:** Just a few minutes ago, these people were talking and laughing... We overestimated our opponents' humanity...

> **星期日：**几分钟前，这些人还有说有笑。我们高估了对手的人性……

- ▸ Are they dead...?

**Ashveil:** They can't die. Hope that puts you at ease.

> **不死途：**它们不会死，希望这能让你轻松些。

- ▸ Got tricked by a scoundrel.

**Sunday:** Sorry, I'm the one to blame.

> **星期日：**抱歉，是我的问题

- ▸ Can I get Lan to glance my way?

**Ashveil:** ...You'd better pray THEY don't look this way.

> **不死途：**…你最好祈祷，祂别看这里。

**Ashveil:** This is a matter of life and death, we don't have time here to be sentimental. Otherwise, there will only be more casualties.

> **不死途：**生死攸关，容不得我们多愁善感。否则只会有更多人罹难。

**Ashveil:** ...No. It's probably more like... wanting to die, but being unable to.

> **不死途：**…不，应该是想死都死不了。

**Broadcast:** "I used to be a fan of the Nameless too. I'd excitedly tell everyone: They're true heroes!"

> **广播：**「我以前也是无名客的粉丝，会激动地和每一个人说：他们是真正的英雄！」

**Broadcast:** "But look how that turned out. They don't care at all about whether the arcadia lives or dies."

> **广播：**「可结果呢？乐园的死活，他们根本不在乎。」

**Broadcast:** "What do we get for our sincerity? The Astral Express just smugly declares: Another trail to blaze!"

> **广播：**「真心换来了什么？星穹列车只会得意洋洋地说：又一场『开拓』！」

**Broadcast:** "(Trailblazer), I ask you: What gives you the right to call yourself a Trailblazer!?"

> **广播：**「开拓者，我问你：你有什么资格自封开拓者？！」

**Himeko:** (Trailblazer), are you okay?

> **姬子：**开拓者，还好吗？

- ▸ Taken out of context... I'm speechless.

**(Trailblazer):** Twisting the facts. That's the only move she's got. I've seen it all before.

> **开拓者：**歪曲事实，她只会用这招，早见识过了。

## 194　Happiness, or a Fatal Ailment　幸福，或致死的疾病

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Seafeld TV Tower　|　角色：Trailblazer; Sunday; Ashveil; Dan Heng; Blade; Fulwish; Phantasmoon; Silver Wolf LV.999; Sparxie; Magnus; Aeon ★ Aha; Zhongshan; Evanescia; March 7th; Welt; Shuhu; Dan Heng • Imbibitor Lunae; Jing Yuan; The Herta; Cyrene; Acheron; Phainon; Collier

### Investigate the giant flower

> *The blossoms on the branch are not always a blessing of rebirth. Sometimes, they are but poisonous thorns in disguise. Regaining her composure amidst lies and deception, she discovered it was all nothing more than a crude joke dedicated to the Fools. Thus, from the ashes and despair, a flower of evil blooms.*

[Recap] According to Fulwish, after the Herald of Death incident, she received care and support from many people around her. With everyone's encouragement, Fulwish regained her hope in life and considered herself the pride of Arcadia.

- ▸ Haha... Hahaha...

> ▸ 哈哈…哈哈哈……

> **满愿：**生命苦短，为什么不多笑笑？我们可都是受阿哈赐福的人。

> **满愿：**看各位脸色铁青，最后，让我再讲一个笑话吧。

### Face Fulwish and refute her perspective

### Watch Sparxie's stream

> *Sparxie? She is still streaming at a moment like this? Some people just love to watch the world burn. Wait a second, the person appearing on the stream is actually Cinderborne from the Daybreak Squadron!?*

[Recap] In Sparxie's livestream, Cinderborne encouraged the public to take action and join the rescue efforts, while Nihilux told everyone to pull themselves together in the face of disaster. Otherwise, she'd draw their cowardly faces into her comics. The people of Planarcadia hadn't given up on saving themselves.

**Cinderborne:** "I am... Cinderborne of the Daybreak Squadron. I'm currently borrowing Miss Sparxie's livestream channel."

> **虚照：**「说到漫画—一绒绒号最新作『谒神！二相乐园之卷』已经完成！发售日定于——」

### Team up with your companions and put an end to this farce

> *Cinderborne... It is truly a relief to see him find his footing again after that tragedy. That is right. "Planarcadia" was never meant to be a place for tears of sorrow. Because there will always be someone who still believes in justice and ideals, someone who looks forward to hope and tomorrow.*

[Recap] To put an end to the disaster, Blade proposed drawing Shuhu's flesh onto himself. To increase the plan's chances of success, Sunday offered to use tuning to help Blade and all the victims maintain their sanity. In the name of the Synwish Syndicate, Sunday used his tuning to send a request to the people of arcadia, calling upon everyone to offer up their "fear."

**Blade:** It seems this world is not completely "incurable" yet.

> **刃：**看来，这个世界离「无可救药」还差一步。

**Sunday:** Don't tell me... you have a plan?

> **星期日：**莫非，你有什么办法？

**Ashveil:** Halovian kid, don't do anything stupid. Do you have any idea how many infected people there are?

**Sunday:** Mr. Ashveil, there will always be people who do stupid things.

> **星期日：**不死途先生，永远会有做傻事的人。

> **星期日：**这段时间，我一直在想：二相乐园何以得名？和匹诺康尼一样，它外表光鲜亮丽，内部却暗流涌动。

**Sunday:** But now I understand. Stasis can never bring happiness. Here, all answers are accepted, and all possibilities are allowed.

> **星期日：**但现在我知道了。静止不可能带来幸福，这里认可所有答案，允许任何可能。

**Sunday:** I once sought to put an end to suffering, and for that very reason...

There's still hope!

[Recap] Through Sunday's tuning, all Shuhu cells across Planarcadia surged toward Blade, driving him into a struggle against the Shuhu within his body. He nearly lost control, but Dan Heng stepped in just in time to help. In the end, Blade absorbed them all. Shuhu's giant tree form manifested, and Silver Wolf altered the parameters of reality to compress it into Blade's body.

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Sunday:** Sweet dreams always come to an end.

> **星期日：**美梦总会醒来。

**Sunday:** This time... I will defend everyone's right to feel pain!

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Pearl:** The ink has not run dry, but the painted scroll is on the verge of tearing.

> **真珠：**笔落未绝，绘卷却快要被撕裂了。

**Pearl:** I never thought the one who'd show up would be you.

> **真珠：**我从没想过，来的人会是你。

*(Cutscene ends)*

[Recap] Kuchiba broke into Pearluxe Tower, demanding to know the IPC's true purpose after years of scheming in Planarcadia. As it turned out, the terrifying entity sealed away here by the Phantasmoon Games was none other than the divine remains of Oroboros, the Voracity.

**Kuchiba:** I'm flattered I managed to slip into your blind spot.

> **朽叶：**能闯入你的盲区，是我的荣幸。

**Pearl:** With everyone's eyes fixed on Fulwish, Pearluxe Tower's security really isn't what it used to be...

> **真珠：**所有人都将目光投向满愿，珠星大厦的安保是大不如前了……

**Pearl:** But that doesn't explain how a mere security officer could sneak in. Answer me this, Miss Kuchiba: Just what sort of secret are you hiding beneath that "mask" of yours?

> **真珠：**但也不是一位治安官能够潜入的。回答我：朽叶小姐，你的「面具」下藏着什么秘密？

**Pearl:** A Masked Fool? A spy from the Marketing Development Department? Or... the ninth Supplicant?

> **真珠：**一位假面愚者？市场开拓部的间谍？还是…第九名谒者？

**Kuchiba:** As of right now, I'm still a security officer for the Department of Aberration Defense, and just as dutiful as you are. All I want is for the Phantasmoon Games to continue.

> **朽叶：**截至目前，我依旧是异常防御部的治安官，和你一样尽责，只希望幻月游戏继续下去。

**Kuchiba:** And as for how much I'm willing to divulge during this conversation... that depends entirely on your sincerity.

> **朽叶：**而在这场谈话中，我愿意吐露多少，也取决于…您的真诚。

**Pearl:** What do you want to know?

> **真珠：**你想问什么？

**Kuchiba:** I know the IPC has been plotting in Planarcadia for years, and it certainly isn't for the authority of the Elation.

> **朽叶：**我知道，公司在乐园谋划多年，绝不是为了什么「欢愉」的权柄。

**Kuchiba:** What you're trying to lay your hands on is the darkest secret of this world... a horror sealed away by the Aeonic game...

> **朽叶：**你们试图染指的，是这个世界最黑暗的秘密，被星神游戏封印的恐怖之物……

**Kuchiba:** ...

> **朽叶：**……

**Kuchiba:** The remains of the Aeon Oroboros are on Ahatopia, right?

> **朽叶：**奥博洛斯的神骸就在哈托彼亚，对吗？

*(Visitor Verification)*

(Silver Wolf LV.999 has become a visitor to the Express. You might come across Silver Wolf LV.999 when you return to the Express)
